It was just idle chatter, but thanks to it, he felt a little better. Perhaps Tang Chan also had the same thought when he said those words.

“I know I need to rest. Let us just finish this work and rest.”

“I heard that more than ten times already.”

Tang Chan, who was about to say something, finally sighed and stopped himself.

What could he do?

Resting didn’t mean that the work would vanish. If one postponed the day’s work, they would only have more work to do tomorrow. There was no such thing as half-assing now.

But one could not expect Tang Gunak to do such half-hearted work. That was something the Tang family would never do.

‘I have to figure out a way....’

It was at that moment that resentment crept back into Tang Chan’s heart.

“Father, no, Lord!”

Burst!

Tang Pae kicked the door open and rushed in.

Looking at his thoughtful face, Tang Gunak instinctively felt something bad had happened and jumped up from his place. His eyes, which held brief comfort, turned cold instantly.

“What is happening?”

Tang Pae waved his hand as if it wasn’t true after seeing Tang Gunak’s reaction. But he didn’t seem to be able to speak easily and just continued to smile numerous times.

“... speak slowly. What happened?”

Even in the midst of all this, Tang Gunak maintained his composure and could be called a model of a lord leading the family.

That was certainly the case until the answer came out.

“Mount Hua has come out!”

“What?”

In an instant, Tang Gunak jumped over the desk and ran to Tang Pae, grabbing him by the collar.

“What now?”

Tang Chan’s mouth dropped.

Of course, it was shocking that Tang Gunak grabbed Tang Pae by the collar, but what was even more shocking was what happened before that.

‘He j-jumped over the desk?’

That Tang Gunak?

The one who always insisted on maintaining a proper form as a family?

“Kuak! Kuak! L-Lord! Let go of...”

“Ah! Sorry.”

Tang Gunak was startled and let go of Tang Pae’s collar, which he had been holding.

“What place did you say it was?”

“M-Mount Hua! Mount Hua sent us a communication. Here, the letter is right here....”

As soon as Tang Pae took the letter out of his robe, Tang Gunak snatched it away like a hawk.

Clack!

And without a moment’s hesitation, he tore open the envelope and took out the paper inside.

“....”

An expression that was hard to describe appeared on Tang Gunak’s face as he silently checked the contents of the letter.

It seemed absurd... or maybe even shocking. However, that strange expression soon turned into a gentle smile.

“W-what does it say?”

“Look at it.”

Tang Chan accepted the letter that Tang Gunak handed him. The letters written on the large piece of paper were barely a single line long.

-Why are you struggling for nothing? Just throw everything away and come to Mount Hua first.

“Huh....”

A squeak sound came from Tang Chan’s mouth.

“Hehehe.....”

For a moment, he doubted his eyes, thinking that he had seen it wrong, but the more he thought about it, the more he realized that it couldn’t have been wrong.

Who in the world could send such a letter to the head of the Sichuan Tang family? Even the head of Shaolin couldn’t dare send a single letter to the head of the Sichuan Tang family and tell them to come.

Only one person could do it in this world, Chung Myung.

“... What will you do?”

“Hmmm.”

As the smile on Tang Gunak’s lips grew deeper, Tang Chan found himself smiling back. It seemed like the first time in years he had seen Tang Gunak with this expression.

It wasn’t a smile that showed he could escape from these suffocating situations now that Mount Hua had returned. Tang Gunak wasn’t the kind of person who would smile so brightly just because he could throw away his work.

That smile right there was the smile of someone who confirmed the health and well-being of a close friend he had lost contact with for a long time.

“Anyway, I cannot stop him.”

Tang Gunak shook his head.

Right.

“Are you leaving?”

Another loud voice was heard from the outside. Tang Gunak, who could guess the owner of the voice, turned his head.

“Enter.”

The door opened without delay, and a person rushed in.

“Did you receive the letter?”

Green Forest King, Im So-Byeong, asked without even greeting him. Tang Gunak silently handed the letter he received.

“As you can see.”

“... Ugh.”

Im So-Byeong groaned after checking the contents.

“Did you receive it?”

“Please check it.”

Im So-Byeong also handed Tang Gunak the letter he was holding.

-Take all the ledgers and come to Mount Hua.

“... Hahaha.”

It was even more concise than the letter Tang Gunak received.

“Mount Hua has finally stepped out.”

At Tang Gunak’s mumbling, everyone exchanged glances with each other, feeling a sense of emotion.

“Ugh.”

However, only Im So-Byeong was frowning.

“It seems that the Green Forest King isn’t happy.”

“It isn’t that I am not happy...”

When Tang Gunak asked casually, Im So-Byeong grabbed his head.

“I don’t know how many of the ledgers, which have been accumulated over the past three years, are there, and he is asking me to bring them all in! I kept sending copies to the Eunha merchant union to avoid this!”

“....”

“Five carts will start first! Five carts!”

“... calm down.”

“Oh my. What...”

Im So-Byeong groaned.

“He is making people suffer like this as soon as he steps out, so I am worried about what will happen in the future. If we meet face to face, he will scream.”

Im So-Byeong shivered as if trembling.

However, Tang Gunak, who was watching him, smiled broadly.

“You look happy for someone who has worries.”

Im So-Byeong touched his face at those words. He could clearly feel the corners of his lips curling up under his fingertips.

Im So-Byeong, who was slightly embarrassed, bit back at Tang Gunak for no reason.

“... But still, do you think I should come?”

Tang Gunak covered his mouth with his fist and coughed lightly. He realized belatedly that he was unusually excited.

Up until then, they had done what they had to do. However, it was also true that even when things went well, they couldn’t help feeling suffocated for three years.

The moment they received Chung Myung’s letters, they could clearly see what the suffocation that had been tormenting them had been.

“What should we do?”

“What can we do?”

Tang Gunak smiled brightly.

“If he tells us to come, we should go. What power do we have to refuse the words of Mount Hua’s Sword Saint?”

“... Since the Lord says so, I will have to be dragged along without fail. Ugh.”

Tang Gunak turned and looked at Tang Pae.

“We head to Mount Hua.”

“Yes, Lord. But... who will hold the position of Lord if you move?”

“Hmmm.”

Tang Gunak recalled the words written on the letter, ‘right away.’

“Tell the servants to stay back and not come out unless needed.”

“That could be a problem.”

“I know.”

Tang Gunak nodded.

“But Mount Hua’s Sword Saint isn’t the type of person to bother writing unnecessary words on a letter.”

“... Right.”

Im So-Byeong nodded as if he completely agreed with those words.

“You seem to have something in mind, so I will do as you say.”

“Yes, Lord. I will.”

“We leave.”

“A-already? No matter how urgent things are, leaving this swiftly?”

Tang Gunak spoke to him with an expressionless face.

“I need to check with my own eyes.”

“Check?”

Tang Gunak turned his head. Through the door that Im So-Byeong had opened just a moment ago, the blue sky was clearly visible. He mumbled in a small voice.

“How much they have changed in the past three years.”

An unconcealed sense of anticipation rose.

“I wonder if that bad-tempered imoogi really has come back as a dragon.”

No one opened their mouths at those words.

Only Im So-Byeong sighed, hitting his head with the fan.

“Even if he became a dragon, he would still be an evil dragon....”

“There is nothing we can do about that.”

Tang Gunak said while looking at Tang Pae.

“Then I will go.”

“Uh? Father? What?”

Tang Pae was still flustered, but Tang Gunak quickly left the room without even listening to the answer. He disappeared in an instant, and Tang Pae lowered his head.

Im So-Byeong shook his head and patted the kid on the shoulder.

“... you must be having a hard time.”

“He isn’t that kind of person...”

A strange place where the next head of the Tang family received comfort from the Green Forest King.

This was the Yangtze River.

Swish.

Even the moon hid behind the clouds, and a dark night fell. A black shadow slowly walked towards the warehouse at the top of Eunha.

He looked around and hid secretly in the shadows, avoiding the eyes of those passing by.

His martial arts skills seemed quite strong, and even the warriors of the merchant guilds, who must have trained quite a bit, could not detect his presence and passed by him calmly.

“...”

He waited for the warriors to completely pass in the darkness and cautiously approached the warehouse. His eyes flashed ominously as he looked at the large lock on the door.

Kiikik.

The man placed his finger gently on the keyhole of the lock. He held that position for a moment then took his hand away and took out a thin wire from his robe, bending it here and there.

The wire, folded into a shape, went into the keyhole.

And after a while.

Click.

The lock opened naturally without a problem.

He carefully opened the lock, looked around once more, fixed his mask, and then crept into the warehouse.

Swish. Swish.

The sound of the cloth laid on the floor to prevent the goods from being damaged quietly spread out.

The man, who succeeded in quietly closing the door, looked around the warehouse with sharp eyes. It was filled with all sorts of things, but he didn’t seem to be searching around rashly, so it seemed like he had something else in mind.

After a while, he quietly closed his eyes in the middle of the warehouse, as if he couldn’t find what he was looking for.

And then...

Blink.

A dazzling light emanated from the man’s eyes as he opened them.

‘Over there.’

He walked ahead without hesitation and lightly tapped the box stored in the innermost, coolest place with the back of his hand.

Thud! Thud!

The corners of the man’s lips, hidden under the mask, curled up.

‘This is it.’

He was careful with his hands as he unwrapped the cloth covering the box. When the box was fully revealed, he grabbed the tightly nailed lid and applied force.

Crack. Crack.

That was the moment when the lid of the box was finally ripped off.

Clack!

The warehouse door, which had been closed, opened violently, and a group of warriors rushed in.

“Stop moving!”

The one who opened the lid flinched. The two eyes revealed through the mask contorted.

“... damn it.”

His hands moved before his head. As soon as he confirmed there was a problem, the man stuffed the contents of the box into his arm sleeves and kicked the ground.

“Catch him!”

Several people with drawn swords rushed at him like lightning, but the man leaped up faster than those who were chasing him. He intended to escape by breaking through the ceiling.

But at that moment, a golden light quickly rose above the masked man’s head.

“Ohhh!”

Hae Yeon, who had flown, threw a fist of resentment at the masked man. The masked man flinched for a moment and tried to change direction by flipping his body in the air. But at that moment, Baek Cheon immediately pulled out his sword and attacked the masked man with his sword qi.

“Kuak!”

The masked man’s eyes became even more distorted.

He could fight if he wanted to, but if he stalled, more would come at him. He shouldn’t fight them one by one but should pull himself out....

That was the moment.

Clack!

He heard the sound of metals entangling each other from below, and then a large iron net flew in and covered the man.

“Ack!”

The masked man, crushed by the net, fell down.

“Hold him tight!”

“Don’t let go!”

“Caught him, this brat!”

Yoon Jong and Jo Gul held the ends of the net tightly and pinned him to the ground. The man, who was struggling for a moment, glared at those who had subdued him with poisonous eyes.

“This...”

A snapping sound came from the man’s mouth. Baek Cheon slowly approached him, looked down with cold eyes, and spoke.

“...take this off this man’s mask.”

“Yes, sasuk.”

Jo Gul reached into the net and removed the mask, revealing a very familiar face.

“Kuaak!”

Those who saw the face sighed in unison.

The masked man, or rather Chung Myung, glared at Hae Yeon with eyes full of resentment.

“That damn bastard! Did you really hit us?”

“...Amitabha! It is all a misunderstanding.”

“Misunderstanding? Misunderstanding, acckk! Ah, really? Misunderstanding? If you misunderstand twice, you could kill someone! Misunderstanding?”

“Ahem.”

Hae Yeon turned his head slightly, feeling shy. Baek Cheon saved him from further embarrassment.

“No matter what....”

He frowned, unable to comprehend the situation.

“Even if you want to drink, is it worth robbing it from the merchant house? Uh? You insane bastard!”

Just then, a pure white bottle rolled out of Chung Myung’s sleeve.

When Yoon Jong snatched the bottle, Chung Myung glared at him and shouted in a hurry.

“I haven’t even sipped it yet!”

Baek Cheon shook his head. Then he turned his gaze and praised Tang Soso.

“...the net is effective like you said.”

“You have to use a net to catch animals. It wouldn’t be of any use if they had swords.”

“Even if he isn’t in the right mind, he wouldn’t draw the sword and swing it at his sahyungs.”

“He swung it?”

“...let us just say he isn’t in the right mind.”

Baek Cheon crouched down in front of Chung Myung, looking at the situation behind him as if being rude.

“Please act like a human, okay? Where do you think this is...”

“No, then give me money!”

“....”

“I don’t have a penny! I cannot go out! I cannot even drink! Why am I even training for?”

“...you are a Taoist, Chung Myung.”

And a Taoist was someone who trained in the Way of Tao.

At that moment, Baek Cheon’s head drooped as if he had no strength to say anything.

Pung!

A refreshing sound rang out as Yoon Jong thoughtlessly opened the bottle of alcohol.

Instantly, a fragrant scent of alcohol spread through the warehouse.

Gulp.

Baek Cheon’s Adam’s apple moved noticeably. He gulped without realizing it and turned his head in surprise, only to see Chung Myung’s eyes completely red and drooling.

“... that....”

Even the others couldn’t take their eyes off the bottle.

Realizing he might have made a mistake, Yoon Jong quickly put the cork back onto the bottle.

“....”

“....”

There was an odd silence.

The first one to break the silence was, of course, the bravest man of Mount Hua, Jo Gul.

“Well... actually, Chung Myung did his part, but... wouldn’t one bottle of alcohol be alright to take?”

Of course, Chung Myung just watched the battle from behind, but no one pointed it out.

“Ah, right. Actually, if he endured this much with sahyung, he must have been through a lot. It’s strange that a tiger doesn’t go crazy when it’s tied up for so long and only given grass to eat instead of meat. Right?”

Tang Soso quickly took Jo Gul’s hand.

“Hmm. But we haven’t even paid tribute to the souls of the victims yet....”

“Isn’t it normal to drink at a funeral?”

Yoon Jong, who was trying to politely object, glared at Jo Gul, who suddenly interrupted. However, he was too speechless at this logic.

“Of course, you shouldn’t be drinking too much, right? But the right amount. Maybe one bottle... wouldn’t we just show gratitude to the dead?”

Baek Cheon was about to cut off Jo Gul, saying it was ridiculous.

“... we can have a drink while watching over.”

“S-samae?”

Baek Cheon looked back at Yu Yiseol with a face that seemed like he’d been hit on the back of his head. However, she clearly avoided his gaze.

“Sasuk!”

“Uh?”

Jo Gul spoke firmly, his expression as serious as if he were facing a life-or-death battle.

“...make the decision.”

“A wise decision.”

“....”

Baek Cheon’s trembling eyes were fixed on the bottle of alcohol that Yoon Jong held.

To be honest, the ones who really needed alcohol weren’t Chung Myung, but them. Chung Myung must have sought out and drunk the alcohol Hwang Jongi had given him while training.

After leaving Mount Hua and entering the mountains, Chung Myung must have had a hard time, but in any case, the others had endured the longest.

‘N-no, even so...’

Baek Cheon shook his head, trying to demonstrate his superhuman patience and reason as the next Sect leader. Still, the gazes of the Five Swords pierced Baek Cheon like fiery arrows.

‘These bastards.’

Their decision was frozen. They had already made up their minds.

“Ahem.”

Baek Cheon coughed with a distressed face and wiped the corner of his lips. A faint wet spot appeared on the sleeve.

“Just one bottle then...”

At that moment, everyone raised their heads at the small voice from ahead.

“...monk?”

Before long, Hae Yeon was lifting the box Chung Myung had opened.

“O-One bottle...”

“Uh?”

Hae Yeon asked, her large eyes sparkling brightly.

“...it is nothing.”

Jo Gul and Yoon Jong approached Baek Cheon and whispered softly.

“Let’s sneak out first.”

“If we’re going to do it, let’s eat outside the merchant manor. We might get caught.”

“We need to erase the traces.”

“Ahem.”

Jo Gul and Yoon Jong discussed Yu Yiseol interfering, while Baek Cheon coughed innocently.

A feeble voice escaped from Chung Myung’s mouth as he blankly watched this.

“...they are growing up well.”

Get rid of the net, you brats!

Baek Cheon, Yu Yiseol, Yoon Jong, Jo Gul, Chung Myung, Tang Soso, and Hae Yeon.

If any martial artists saw these people sitting together, they would all be amazed.

Mount Hua’s Sword, Chung Myung, received recognition from Jang Ilso, surpassing the skilled individuals of Mount Hua, including the Righteous Sword, Baek Cheon, who garnered attention as the next Sect leader. Then there are Mount Hua’s Five Swords, incomparable to their peers of other sects, and Hae Yeon, the best warrior in Shaolin.

These individuals were truly worthy of being called the future leaders in murim. It would certainly be impressive to see such people sitting together.

It would certainly be impressive. It would be shocking...

“Kuaak!”

“Woah! This is sticking to me!”

“Is it because it is expensive alcohol? Or is it because it has been a long time since I’ve had alcohol?”

“One more glass!”

“Taoist, pour it for me! Me!”

Baek Cheon felt indescribable as he watched Tang Soso and Yu Yiseol fill the glasses with alcohol.

‘Is this alright?’

No matter what, stealing alcohol from someone else’s warehouse was...

Gulp! Gulp! Gulp! Gulp!

“...”

But he soon ended up laughing happily.

From the moment he realized that the person drinking the bottle vigorously was not Chung Myung but Hae Yeon, such worries were meaningless.

‘Let us not think about it.’

But...

“Ugh!”

Ah, the alcohol tasted so good. It really stuck to the throat.

Baek Cheon, who had let go of his thoughts, downed the glass of alcohol and looked back at Chung Myung with a relaxed face.

Gulp. Gulp. Gulp.

He was drinking.

Chung Myung was pouring alcohol as if he had finally found a house after wandering around the desert and drinking water.

“Kuaaak!”

Finally looking at Chung Myung’s expression, he pulled the empty bottle from his mouth with a wet sound... he couldn’t help but think, what is happiness in life?

“Ah. I really thought I would die.”

“... No one has died from not drinking.”

“I almost got one for the first time.”

“... I said it wouldn’t happen.”

I should just stop talking.

“Rather....”

Baek Cheon opened his mouth while looking at Chung Myung with a serious expression.

“What do you think will happen to you?”

“Hmmm? What are you talking about all of a sudden? You’re drinking well.”

“I mean the Four Sect Alliance.”

Baek Cheon’s face naturally became clouded as he thought of them.

“I heard that the situation there is not so good either. Do you think they can handle this?”

Chung Myung wiped a drop of alcohol from his chin.

“Hmm.”

He then quietly placed the bottle of alcohol to the side. His eyes, which had been raised, were slightly dim.

“Hmm.”

Chung Myung lightly scratched his cheek, exhaling deeply.

“... I was just thinking about having fun.”

“Don’t mess around.”

“I am serious.”

“....”

When Baek Cheon looked at him with indescribable contempt, Chung Myung waved his hand with an annoyed expression.

“What are you looking at me like that for? It is not for me to decide.”

“Uh?”

“Those ones should decide.”

Everyone knew who Chung Myung was referring to when he said, ‘Those ones’.

“... Four Precepts Alliance.”

“Right.”

When Baek Cheon spoke as if groaning, Chung Myung nodded lightly.

“That is certainly true, but...”

Yoon Jong tilted his head and said.

“But if what branch leader Hong said is true, then there could be a situation where the Four Precepts Alliance cannot move because of internal problems.”

“That won’t happen.”

“Uh?”

Chung Myung shrugged.

“Of course, there will be internal problems. But they wouldn’t waste time because of that. He is not the type of person who would let that get in the way.”

Everyone nodded in agreement.

They also knew that the leaders of the Shenzhou Five Clans were no ordinary people. Didn’t they already witness their strength with their own eyes in the Yangtze River?

They were not pushed back an inch by the leaders of the Nine Great Sects and the Five Great Families who commanded the world. No, in some ways, they were even overwhelming.

But...

‘Will Jang Ilso be overthrown by them?’

Even if he was caught by them, he wasn’t the kind of person who would waste time there. Jang Ilso would cut off his own ankle and go on his way.

Baek Cheon’s face hardened as he thought this far.

He wasn’t thinking this way because he was taking the leaders of the Shenzhou Five Clans lightly. It was because Jang Ilso’s presence was so much greater than theirs.

“If it is Jang Ilso, then...”

Chung Myung nodded and said calmly.

“And think about it. Wouldn’t Jang Ilso know that we know, that the North knows, and that the Justice faction knows everything?”

“...Of course, he would know it.”

“Yes, he would know. If he knew, he would prepare for it. There is no way he wouldn’t think about what I would do.”

Baek Cheon looked at Chung Myung with a mix of respect and frustration.

It was a clear fact that Chung Myung hated Jang Ilso more than anyone else in murim. But when he heard it like this, he started to think that maybe the person Chung Myung acknowledged the most in the world was also Jang Ilso.

‘It is a little annoying.’

Who else could make that damned guy say something like that?

“And he has already moved.”

“Uh? What do you mean?”

When Jo Gul asked with eyes wide, Chung Myung chuckled.

“Is it really a coincidence that those Evil Sect people invaded Shaanxi at this moment?”

“...Did Jang Ilso plan that too?”

Yoon Jong shook his head.

“That seems like an insane idea. I questioned the Jiangxi Seven Killers, but Gal Cheon-rip had nothing to do with the Four Precepts Alliance. And he wasn’t the type to listen to Jang Ilso in the first place.”

Chung Myung stares at Yoon Jong and shakes his head.

“Sahyung.”

“Uh?”

“Sahyung is good, but the problem is that you are too kind.”

“...Is that an insult?”

Considering who those words were from, it was definitely not praise. Absolutely not.

“It is a lowlife move to make things happen by taking action yourself. A true master creates situations where things happen without you having to do anything.”

Chung Myung took a sip from a new bottle of alcohol and continued speaking.

“No matter how big the Southern land is, if Jang Ilso had wanted to, he could have cut off those guys before they even came up to the North. It is even stranger that he’d let them go when he’d already occupied the Yangtze River.”

“...Then Jang Ilso sent those people to Shaanxi on purpose?”

“Yes.”

“Why?”

Baek Cheon asked with a puzzled look. Chung Myung shrugged his shoulders.

“I don’t know that.”

“...”

At that, everyone’s eyes dimmed with subtle contempt. Chung Myung’s face twisted at their reaction.

“No, wait, am I some kind of mind reader? How can I know what that crazy bastard is even thinking!”

“You should know.”

“Insane people communicate well with each other.”

“Even if everyone else in the world doesn’t know, you should know him.”

“... these damned people.”

Chung Myung gulped down his drink as if his throat was burning.

Actually, he thought it was a joke, but his eyes were darker than before as if the words he hadn’t said had sunk into his eyes.

‘Jang Ilso...’

He put down the bottle and opened his mouth again, wiping his lips.

“You just need to know what is certain.”

“What is certain?”

Chung Myung nodded.

“He is moving.”

The room became quiet.

Everyone knew it. Wasn’t it because they had thought of this possibility that they endured the difficult training?

Baek Cheon nodded.

“We should prepare before that so that we can deal with Jang Ilso no matter what he does.”

“Well...”

Chung Myung glanced at Baek Cheon’s words and laughed.

“Plan anything so that the water stays fresh, right?”

“Right.”

“... it wasn’t fresh water, but spoiled one.”

“Ah, you mean when sahyung threw up?”

“You are the one who threw up, moron!”

Yoon Jong kicked Jo Gul with his foot. However, Jo Gul had definitely grown over the past three years, and now he showed determination to say what he wanted to say even when he got hit.

“I just puked a little; sahyung threw up everything you had for breakfast!”

“When did I do that, you moron!”

Yoon Jong protested with a flushed face. However, no one wanted to tease Yoon Jong about that. Actually, in the midst of such harsh training, such a thing was just a small incident that could be laughed off.

“Rather than preparing, we should organize it.”

“Organize?”

“Yes. Organize it.”

Chung Myung slightly curled his lips into a smile.

“We should organize the situation that’s turning into a mess so that when that bastard Jang Ilso comes running with his eyes wide open, it’ll be easier to handle. Above all... we need to do something about those damned Nine Sects.”

As he brought up the Nine Sects, Chung Myung’s face began to boil red again. Hae Yeon flinched quietly and looked away.

“So there’s no need to think about it from now.”

Chung Myung’s voice was filled with confidence.

“The time for us to fight will come soon.”

Everyone’s faces hardened, and they nodded.

As the atmosphere died down in an instant, Chung Myung lifted the bottle of alcohol in his hand and shook it gently.

“So let us drink today.”

“That isn’t a bad idea.”

“He sometimes says the right things.”

The atmosphere quickly heated up again. Chung Myung, leaning back against the wall, snickered as he watched everyone competitively emptying their bottles.

‘We deserve it.’

In the past, Chung Myung would have cursed the young bastards for only playing around, but such words did not suit them now. They had endured the harsh training for the past three years without complaint.

Even Chung Myung of the past wouldn’t have been able to endure that training at their age. Of course, they would have done it if needed...

-Who? You? Youuuuu?

“Ah, that man, seriously.”

“Uh?”

“... No, it is nothing.”

Chung Myung quietly contorted his face.

Anyway.

Those who had driven themselves harshly needed a break, and that was only right. It wasn’t like Chung Myung stole alcohol from the warehouse because he wanted to drink...

-You are being a liar.

“No, this man is seriously-!”

“Why do you keep doing that?”

“... no.”

Chung Myung sighed deeply, unable to vent his frustrations.

‘Rather...’

Then, suddenly, a gloomy aura appeared in his eyes.

‘He is definitely up to something.’

It didn’t seem like Jang Ilso would start a war across the Yangtze River. Jang Ilso was the type who created a battlefield advantageous to him and manipulated it as he pleased.

The fact that the Evil Sect had been quiet recently suggests they were up to something. A decisive move to resolve the internal clashes within the Four Precepts Alliance and see the war against the Nine Great Sects to victory.

“... well, it makes sense.”

Chung Myung mumbled quietly, twisting the corners of his lips.

No matter how mysterious the strategy was, it was meaningless if one lacked skills. That was something Chung Myung had felt to his bones at the Yangtze River.

Now, Chung Myung... no, Mount Hua would show Jang Ilso what it meant.

“... sasuk. That kid is laughing horribly?”

“I told you to just leave him alone. It seems like he is losing it again. Things seem to be getting worse recently.”

“If he gets crazier than now, it will be hard to see him as a person.”

No... let Chung Myung do what he wanted.

Yes.

“The compensation is all done.”

“Hmm.”

“The corpses from the village that the Evil Sect people pillaged have all been collected and given funerals, and the survivors have been transferred to the next village. I promised to support them in the name of Mount Hua and the Eunha merchants so they will not be treated badly.”

“I see.”

“I heard that the bodies of the disciples of clan leader Wei Lishan are also being returned to Nanyang.”

Hyun Jong, who had been listening to the report, nodded quietly.

With this, they could say that Mount Hua’s work in Xi’an was finished. It took a little longer than expected, but thanks to that, they were able to finish it more neatly.

“Merchant lord.”

When Hyun Jong called him, Merchant Lord Hwang Jongi straightened his body with a slightly tense expression. He had a feeling that something official was about to be discussed.

“Yes, sect leader.”

Hyun Jong looked at Hwang Jongi and began to speak.

“Thank you for your hard work.”

“N-No, it was nothing.”

When Hwang Jongi waved his hand in embarrassment, Hyun Jong smiled warmly and continued.

“I am not just talking about this incident. Knowing how much the merchant lord suffered while Mount Hua was in charge of the Closed Gate, it has been hard for me to raise my head.”

“S-sect leader.”

Hyun Jong bowed his head to Hwang Jongi.

“I am always grateful for what you have done for Mount Hua, which was lacking.”

Hwang Jongi was startled and tried to stop Hyun Jong.

“Why are you doing this, sect leader! Mount Hua is lacking!? How can you say such a thing! Mount Hua is not lacking at all.”

At those words, Hyun Jong slightly raised his head.

“You said we are not lacking?”

“Y-yes.”

Hyun Jong looked somewhere, then stared at that place and asked again.

“... do you really think so?”

“...”

Hwang Jongi couldn’t open his mouth for a moment.

Because at the end of Hyun Jong’s gaze were the Mount Hua disciples, all banging their heads on the floor.

“Ugh....”

“Head....”

“This little bastard Jo Gul... who would use his internal qi? Do you want to get kicked out?”

“... don’t do this to him again....”

A roar like a lion burst from Hyun Jong’s mouth.

“Aren’t you going to bang your heads straight, you damned bastards!”

These people here were not the disciples of Mount Hua who returned to the Eunha merchant at dawn and got caught. They were the world’s most evil people who drank all the alcohol they could, passed out, and came back in the morning smelling of alcohol...

‘These morons!’

‘How can they not be alright?’

‘I believed these people were sahyung-sajes.’

Hyun Jong’s voice was filled with anger as he addressed the Five Swords, who were glaring at each other like drawn knives.

“The problem hasn’t even been resolved yet! Alcohol? Alcohol? And you’re stealing it from someone else’s warehouse? Are you still Taoists? Are you still Taoists?”

At that moment, Chung Myung raised his hand as if he had been struck on the head.

“What?”

“Ughh... hehe. I did something wrong, but if you think about it, Eunha and we aren’t the only ones...”

“What, now?”

As Hyun Jong’s eyes widened, Hyun Young and Hyun Sang hurriedly grabbed him from both sides and held on.

“Sect leader, calm down!”

“Others are watching you, sect leader!”

“That is the problem!”

Hyun Jong shouted like thunder and glared at the elders as if he was about to devour them.

“Isn’t this because others are watching! I cannot live because I am not this shameless!”

“Ahem!”

“In the past, that guy would cause trouble by himself, but now...”

Everyone, including Baek Cheon, flinched at those words and closed their eyes.

“... I thought he would have matured a bit after suffering for three years, but he’s worse than before! Even worse! What on earth will happen on Mount Hua!”

Hyun Jong, who was so angry that he couldn’t stand it anymore, sighed and turned his head. Then he held Hwang Jongi’s hand and bowed his head.

“Thank you for being such a help to the worthless Mount Hua...”

“...”

Looking at Hyun Jong’s face, which was a mixture of sadness and resentment, Hwang Jongi couldn’t say anything. He had no choice but to keep his mouth shut.

Hyun Jong raised his head after vaguely apologizing for his disciples stealing the alcohol and for showing him their worthless side.

“Hyun Sang, Hyun Young.”

“Yes, sect leader.”

“Get ready right away.”

“Uh?”

“We return to Mount Hua.”

Hyun Sang looked at Hyun Jong with a happy face.

“Now that we have finished the work here, we will have to return to Mount Hua...”

“I cannot stay here any longer because I am so embarrassed! Pack your bags! Now!”

“...”

“Quick!”

“... Yes.”

That was how Mount Hua’s return was suddenly decided.

The inside of Eunha Merchant Manor was crowded with people.

The Xi’an sects, who had heard that Mount Hua was returning, gathered to see them off.

“... Did you come in person?”

“Ahem.”

In response to the question from the clan leader Dan Byung-Rip of the Great Wind Clan, clan leader Nam Ja-Myung coughed loudly.

He pretended to calm his expression and spoke in a solemn tone.

“Even though we belong to different clans, shouldn’t we remember to be grateful? Since we have received a favor from Mount Hua, it is only natural to show respect.”

“For that, you have a lot in your hands.”

“... It is just a token of sincerity.”

“If you show sincerity twice, you will have to pull out the roots of the sect. Today is the first time I found that the Full Moon Clan has such money.”

“T-this man is serious!”

When Nam Ja-Myung glared at him, Dan Byung-Rip turned his head away with a sour look on his face. Although he was teasing him, he also understood Nam Ja-Myung’s feelings.

‘I guess this is an inevitable choice.’

Not only the clans of Xi’an but also the people of Xi’an who received the grace of Mount Hua had come.

Now, everyone in Xi’an knew that those Evil Sect bastards killed innocent villagers on their way to Xi’an. Everyone could easily guess that Xi’an would have suffered the same fate if Mount Hua hadn’t come.

So naturally, Xi’an people’s favor towards Mount Hua soared to an unparalleled level. Three years ago, Mount Hua protected the pride of Shaanxi in the Yangtze River. Now they had saved their lives, so it would be strange if they were not happy.

This meant...

‘Now, if you want to do business in Xi’an, you have no choice but to align with Mount Hua.’

The disciples who were there now might be able to protect them. But now and in the future, why would anyone want to deal with Southern Edge’s clans when there were other sub-clans of Mount Hua, like the Hwa-young clan?

Public sentiment was sometimes cold to the extent of being cruel.

Now, the kids did not speak of the Thirty-Six Sword Movements while swinging their sticks. Since the words “Plum Blossom Sword technique” were coming out of the children’s mouths, the public sentiment in Xi’an was as good as gone.

Even if the Southern Edge were to release its Closed Gate, it would not be able to reverse this.

So, the representative of Southern Edge’s sub-clans, Nam Ja-Myung, would naturally be upset.

‘If I were to clearly switch to Mount Hua, my relationship with Southern Edge would be ruined, and if I were to stay as Southern Edge’s sub-clan, then my name would be ruined.’

A warrior clan that didn’t have disciples was no different from a store that customers didn’t visit.

On the other hand...

“Hello!”

“Ohh, young clan leader!”

“Hehe. I have only seen you for a few days, but you seem to have turned more devoted.”

The other side literally hit the jackpot.

Everyone was impatient because they couldn’t speak to Wei Soheng, who was from the Hwa-young clan. The fact that people were so humble to the next clan leader, who wasn’t even a fully fledged clan leader, showed the position the Hwa-young clan held in Xi’an.

‘Of course. It is the Hwa-young clan.’

The Hwa-young clan was not just a simple sub-sect of Mount Hua. It was a true friend that had stayed by Mount Hua’s side even when no one in the world of murim showed interest.

A friend who stayed by the side of Mount Hua during difficult times was treated much better than a friend who only came in good times.

Those who knew how much Mount Hua values the Hwa-young clan could not help but try to look good.

‘Xi’an has really changed so much.’

No, it wasn’t just Xi’an that has changed.

Now that Mount Hua has returned, all of Kangho must naturally be nervous.

Even before Mount Hua entered their Closed Gate, each of their actions had stirred the Murim world. It was clear that their skills had improved significantly over the past three years, causing quite a storm.

That was when.

“Come out!”

“Mount Hua’s people are coming out!”

As someone shouted, all eyes turned toward them. Indeed, Mount Hua’s swordsmen were seen walking out in a line.

Those watching them gulped momentarily, a strange look in their eyes.

When they had fought here before, they shuddered at the killing intent and presence of Mount Hua’s swordsmen. But now, they didn’t feel that at all.

There was a moderate amount of gentleness and calmness.

‘It is like the Southern Edge.’

‘No, it seems like they are more relaxed than Southern Edge.’

‘Now Mount Hua has truly acquired the dignity of a great sect.’

It was not just skills that made people look up to them. Those who were once again impressed by Mount Hua opened their mouths and greeted Hyun Jong, who was in the lead.

“Sect leader! I heard you are going back to Mount Hua!”

“I don’t know how to repay the favor that Mount Hua and Southern Edge have shown me.”

“We will also visit Mount Hua often. So please stop by Xi’an frequently.”

Hyun Jong, slightly surprised by the crowd of people, smiled gently.

“You are just going home, but I don’t know what to do with myself for sending you off like this.”

“Of course you should.”

Nam Ja-Myung stepped forward and bowed his head to Hyun Jong.

“I would like to thank you on behalf of the Xi’an clans and the common people here for saving the city. I should have come here to thank you long ago.”

“Don’t do this, clan leader.”

Hyun Jong grabbed Nam Ja-Myung’s hand tightly.

“We are no strangers, so if you show this much courtesy, I will feel embarrassed.”

“Sect leader....”

Nam Ja-Myung looked at Hyun Jong blankly at the words, ‘We are no strangers.’

“Would it be impossible for our clan to be friends with Mount Hua? It isn’t necessary to be a clan under Mount Hua to be friends.”

“... would the Southern Edge’s clan also be able to do that?”

Hyun Jong smiled brightly.

“You speak of something so obvious.”

“Ah. Then, Southern Edge too....”

“....”

Hyun Jong smiled brightly and didn’t say anything, so Nam Ja-Myung’s eyes sent warning signals.

In addition, the disciples of the Mount Hua sect standing behind Hyun Jong were all looking at each other as if to say, ‘Not Southern Edge, not that place, you moron!’

“... I was short-sighted; I blurted out the wrong words. Forgive me, Sect Leader.”

“Do not say that.”

At that moment, Dan Byeong-Rip, who was next to him, quickly pushed the man away and asked.

“We are close, right, Sect Leader?”

“Yes, that is true.”

“I understand what you mean.”

When Dan Byeong-Rip nodded, Hyun Jong smiled brightly. Then he said,

“You say that Mount Hua protected Xi’an, but that is wrong.”

“... Uh?”

“If you hadn’t risked your lives to stop the Evil Sect first, the damage would have been too much to handle even when Mount Hua arrived.”

“...”

“You were truly wonderful.”

Dan Byeong-Rip couldn’t continue speaking and looked at Hyun Jong. After hesitating a bit, he finally nodded.

“Thank you, Sect Leader.”

“No need to say it.”

Hyun Jong lightly waved his hand.

“Then I will leave now. I am very eager to speak, but we have also left our sect unattended for too long.”

“Ye-yes! You must do so since you are busy with the reconstruction.”

The disciples, who had been desperate to say a word, soon gave up and cleared the way.

Except for one person.

“Taoist!”

Wei Soheng ran to Chung Myung, who was behind him. However, Chung Myung kept his gaze fixed ahead and didn’t turn around.

“Taoist Chung Myung?”

And without giving Wei Soheng a glance, he motioned for them to move away with his chin.

Wei Soheng, observing the situation, spoke as if he understood.

“... Did you do something again?”

“....”

“Then... how many days is the punishment?”

At that moment, Hyun Jong turned around, making Chung Myung flinch.

“Isn’t the young clan leader asking? Don’t worry about it and answer him.”

“Is that right?”

“...eh.”

Hyun Jong quickly turned his head as if he didn’t want to continue the conversation. Only then did Chung Myung look at Wei Soheng, mumbling.

“What is it?”

“Taoist! Can I visit Mount Hua when I have time?”

“I don’t see any problem... Well, I don’t know. How long will we even be at Mount Hua?”

“Uh?”

“We’re going to get a bit busy now.”

Chung Myung giggled.

“I have been training hard, so I should swing my sword a bit. If not, it will be unfair.”

Wei Soheng nodded quickly, understanding after seeing Chung Myung’s confident face.

“Yes, Taoist. Then I will not bother you.”

“It isn’t a bother.”

Chung Myung placed his hand on Wei Soheng’s head.

“The sub-clan is also Mount Hua.”

“...”

“As Mount Hua has worked hard and gotten stronger, the Hwa-young clan should also get stronger. When I return to Mount Hua, I will quickly establish a method so that the Hwa-young clan can also train well.”

“Then, can I be of help to the Taoist?”

“You are already doing a good job at that.”

Chung Myung smiled.

“Compared to the sahyungs behind me, you are an adult, an adult.”

“This brat is?”

“Yah, I am not denying that, but it doesn’t seem like something you should be saying.”

Chung Myung ignored their words and spoke to Wei Soheng.

“A war so big that even the Hwa-young clan cannot avoid is coming soon. The only thing that can save you in the war is your skills. So do not slack off on the training.”

“I will keep that in mind.”

“Yes.”

Chung Myung chuckled and patted him on the shoulder once again.

“Give my regards to the clan leader.”

“Yes.”

Jo Gul, who was watching Chung Myung address Wei Soheng, frowned slightly and said.

“Why is that brat so kind to him?”

Baek Cheon answered calmly.

“You don’t seem to know much, but he is actually kind with kids.”

“Uh?”

“He is kind to the younger ones. He was like that in the North Sea too.”

“What about us then?”

“Uh? What?”

“When that brat and I first met, we were kids too, but that brat lifted my chin and hit me against the ceiling as soon as he saw me.”

Baek Cheon smiled and answered.

“Gul.”

“Uh?”

“At least he was a kid back then. He was younger than you.”

The voice that explained everything to us one by one was incredibly kind.

“And after all, you know,”

“...”

“He can do that.”

“....”

“That guy is weak to people who are humans.”

“Then, wasn’t I a human?”

“Do I really have to spill it out now?”

Jo Gul, who was about to protest, quietly closed his mouth for a moment. This was because Yoon Jong was looking at him with a look that said, ‘Please say one more thing so that I can shut you up’.

“Then, Taoist, have a good journey!”

“Yes.”

When Wei Soheng returned to his place, Hwa-young clan members lined up around him.

“I pray for the safety of the Mount Hua sect!”

Everyone simultaneously gave a round of applause to see Mount Hua off. The other clans who saw this also lined up in unison and gave their respects to Mount Hua.

Hyun Jong greeted them one by one with a gentle smile and claps.

“Goodbye all!”

As Mount Hua was out of the gate, the waiting civilians all cheered. As they watched Mount Hua walk into the cheers, the disciples of Xi’an clans trembled with a new feeling.

“Clan leader Dan.”

“Yes, Clan leader Nam.”

“What did the sect leader mean by ‘friend’ earlier? I think clan leader Dan has an idea.”

Dan Byeong-Rip smiled at Nam Ja-Myung’s question.

“The sect leader meant the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

“Heavenly Friends Alliance? Ah... friend. Right, that makes sense.”

Whether they were the Southern Edge’s sub-sect or not, everyone who joined the Heavenly Friends Alliance was considered Mount Hua’s friend.

“...Then I guess we should make a move right away....”

“You will join?”

“I want to say that there is no other way to make a living, but...”

The man smiled bitterly.

“To be honest, I also want to be a friend of Mount Hua.”

“I don’t think Southern Edge would like it.”

“Parents cannot be happy with all of their kid’s friends, so I hope they understand.”

Nam Ja-Myung gulped at the implication of those words.

The leaders of the clans who heard the conversation between the two also watched the Mount Hua sect moving away.

And Hwang Jongi, who was observing all of this from behind, smiled brightly.

“Now, there is going to be a lot of work to do.”

However, the work that needed to be done no longer felt arduous.

The disciples of Mount Hua, who had passed through the gates amidst applause, sighed with tired faces.

“...I am exhausted.”

“Why are there so many people around?”

Those who pretended to be calm warriors when others were watching slumped their shoulders as soon as the others disappeared.

“I almost had a sore throat with how much I tensed my shoulders.”

“I kept my eyes open too much, so my eyes hurt.”

“What can we do though? They like us so much.”

They were complaining that it was hard, but they didn’t really look upset. Rather, the smile on their lips suggested they were enjoying the situation itself.

Hyun Jong asked casually.

“If it is that hard, would you like to rest a bit?”

“No!”

“We move quickly, sect leader!”

“We have delayed too much already! We have to get there before the sun sets!”

Mount Hua’s disciples shouted as if they had nothing to think about.

“Guys.”

They all must have felt the same way.

Their bodies hadn’t fully recovered from the long training, so it wasn’t easy to take the long way. Nevertheless, everyone was stamping their feet as if they wanted to run to Mount Hua in one stride.

‘Did we miss it so much?’

The disciples of Mount Hua turned their gazes to the east.

Far away, where five mountains peaked through the horizon.

To Mount Hua, from which they came.

“Then let us go!”

“Yes!”

The disciples of Mount Hua began to run quickly.

With a little excitement and deep longing in their hearts.

Kwang!

“WOAHHH! We arrived!”

“It is Mount Hua!”

“Oh my, mother!”

The disciples of Mount Hua, who had kicked open the gate of the mountain, looked at the pavilions with eyes filled with emotion.

They had climbed up the rugged Mount Hua in one breath, but instead of feeling exhausted, they felt full of energy and vigor.

“Ahh.”

“Mount Hua....”

Everyone looked as if they would burst into tears at any moment.

Hyun Jong smiled broadly.

‘Do they like it so much?’

Well, if you thought about it, it wasn’t so strange.

Most of Mount Hua’s disciples had never been away from Mount Hua for a long time. It was only natural that they would be moved after being away from such a place for two years.

Hyun Jong’s heart warmed.

Didn’t this mean that Mount Hua’s disciples treated this place like their home? The sect leader couldn’t help but be happy....

“Oh my! The roof! Roof!”

“I want to cry!”

“I won’t have to sleep in the grass anymore and get bitten by the bugs!”

“When I wake up in the grass, my back ends up soaking wet, and now that is a goodbye!!”

“Food! I can eat food that tastes like human food now! Food!”

“....”

Hyun Jong covered his mouth with his fist and coughed lightly.

‘This is embarrassing.’

Now that Mount Hua was considered a wealthy sect, he was moved to tears by the fact that his disciples could sleep in a pavilion again. Even the Beggars Union wouldn’t have...

Of course, if one knew their circumstances, it would be natural.

In order to avoid the eyes of outsiders, they went deep into the mountains to train. To put it nicely, they trained like hermits in a deep valley, and to put it badly, they were begging like beggars in the mountains.

After swinging the sword like mad people from morning to night, scraping up whatever they could eat, and sleeping on the dirt for so long, it wasn’t surprising that they were moved to see where they could finally live like humans.

Although he stayed at the Eunha with the kids, it couldn’t have been so comfortable for the disciples.

“Bed! I need to collapse on my bed!”

“Get out of my way! I need to do it first!”

“Don’t we have a diner?”

“We do have one. I’ve been away from it for years.”

“Maybe...”

Just as the disciples were losing their composure, the general of Mount Hua stamped his foot.

Thump!

The disciples all turned to the side.

“Tsk, tsk, tsk.”

Hyun Young clicked his tongue and looked around at the disciples.

“No matter how anxious you are, you have to do what needs to be done.”

Then he gestured at the pavilions with his chin.

“Do you not see it?”

“Uh?”

When the disciples seemed unsure, Hyun Young kindly explained.

“Do you see the white dust that has accumulated on the pavilion there?”

“....”

“I am not asking for anything hard. I wish the renovation work had been finished today, but I am just human, so I don’t hope for it. But!”

Everyone’s faces began to turn pale as Hyun Young started to smile.

“First, we should clean up the dust that has accumulated while we were away.”

‘That devil!’

‘Don’t be so uptight!’

‘Be flexible!’

Hyun Young just shrugged, pretending he heard the protests from the disciples.

“You have something to say?”

“N-No!”

“Not at all!”

“Of course, we agree!”

No matter how strong their martial arts were, no one had yet grown bold enough to defy Hyun Young.

Hyun Young nodded happily and shouted.

“Okay then. Move! Let us finish this quickly!”

“Yes!”

Mount Hua’s disciples ran out.

“Woah....”

Hong Dae-Kwang, who had arrived a little late, stared blankly at Mount Hua.

What would they say if they showed the people below what was happening here? They would probably say that the actions of the sect, which were not common sense in the murim world, needed to be reconsidered.

“Move quickly!”

“I am going! Move!”

“There! Sweep there, I will do it!”

The disciples of Mount Hua were cleaning at an incredible speed. They weren’t just sweeping and wiping; they were carrying buckets as big as people, running to the stream below to draw water, and literally ‘washing’ the pavilion.

Dozens of water buckets flew in succession, spraying water as if rain were pouring down from the sky.

“... Amazing.”

He felt this way when he saw the Tang family before, but wasn’t it a waste of strength for warriors to act as guards and cleaners? If they were used for construction, they could easily do the work of ten skilled workers...

The halls, which had been covered in dust and white smoke, quickly regained their original color.

‘Come to think of it, Mount Hua’s Sword Saint is?’

Other people were cleaning, but it didn’t seem like that person would even clean...

“The dust isn’t cleaned well!”

“Ugh!”

At that moment, he saw Chung Myung mercilessly kicking the butt of his sahyung.

“Ancestral tablet! How can there be dust on the ancestral tablet! Are you going to just wipe the dust off?”

“N-No, we were told to just clean it...”

“Here! Do you not see the dust here? See!”

“... Chung Myung. I cannot see anything.”

“If even a speck of dust remains today, all the sahyungs will die! Hurry up and clean it!”

“Ah, fine.”

Chung Myung, who had taken over the Jade Heavenly Hall, rolled his eyes and shouted like a lion.

He also held a cloth and began to wipe the tablet with zeal.

“Hooo. Hooo!”

Plop. Plop.

“Hoo. Pheww!”

Squeak.

Baek Cheon shook his head as he watched Chung Myung wipe the tablets with the others of Great Mount Hua Sect’s Thirteenth Generation Sect Leader, Chung Mun, engraved on it.

Even madness could be felt in the hand movements as this guy wiped the tablet.

‘Is that kid ill-mannered? Or is he too ill-mannered?’

While kicking his sahyung and sajaes, he still respected his ancestors, whom he had never even met. Where did something go wrong to make such a horrible person act out?

Crack!

“Ack! There is a crack on the tablet! Oh my! Chung Mun sahyungggg! Give me glue! Rice!!!!”

“...insane bastard!”

Baek Cheon, who had stopped thinking, shook his head. Of course, Hong Dae-Kwang was the same.

Blink. Blink.

In just an hour, Mount Hua regained its original look. The tiles, which were stained and dirty, had regained their original color and were shining, and the pillars with walls that were dull and faded had become clear as if they were oiled.

The curtains hung on the long clothesline and swayed gently in the wind.

It was a truly peaceful scene.

“Okay, now...”

Hyun Young looked around with a sharp glance.

The disciples couldn’t take their eyes off Hyun Young, who was tense. Their fates were determined by what he said.

“Well, this much is...”

Hyun Young nodded, not pleased with it, but still accepting it. Everyone let out a silent cheer.

“Sect leader.”

“Yes.”

“The kids have been working hard, so I think it would be good to let them eat and rest comfortably for today.”

“That is a nice thought, but is there anything to eat in Mount Hua now? Since all the servants were sent out, there is no one to cook.”

“I stopped by Hwa-Um village on the way and ordered some food. They should just go down and get it.”

“You did all that has to be done. Heheh.”

As Hyun Jong was about to nod happily, he saw Chung Myung sneaking up behind Hyun Young and whispering.

“Elder. Alcohol, alcohol...”

“This...! Yah, brat! You got so drunk and got in trouble, and now you are speaking about alcohol?”

Hyun Jong shouted, making Hyun Young frown.

“That is that, and this is this! The kids could be a little thirsty!”

“Ughh!”

Just as Hyun Jong was about to say one more thing.

“Alcohol is here.”

A voice was heard from behind, making everyone turn their heads instinctively.

The closed gate of Mount Hua slowly opened, and a person appeared—a middle-aged man holding a white bottle of alcohol in one hand, dressed in green clothing.

“Oh!”

Hyun Jong’s eyes were filled with joy.

“Lord!”

“Father!”

Likewise, those who noticed Tang Gunak couldn’t suppress their joy and shouted loudly. Tang Gunak smiled brightly at the warm reception.

He walked slowly through the gate and bowed to Hyun Jong.

“Greetings to the Alliance Lord.”

“Do not do this now, family lord.”

Hyun Jong quickly grabbed his arms and lifted him up.

“I am a sinner who failed to fulfill my role as the Alliance Lord and passed the burden to you. I cannot accept this greeting.”

Tang Gunak smiled.

“It doesn’t seem like you failed to fulfill your role.”

“But... how did you get here so quickly?”

Hyun Jong asked with a surprised face, and Tang Gunak answered, looking embarrassed.

“I came running right after receiving the letter. When I heard that Mount Hua opened their gates, my body felt so hot that I couldn’t stay there anymore.”

“You did well coming. Welcome.”

Hyun Jong’s eyes were filled with joy and regret at the same time.

He already knew how much Tang Gunak suffered while Mount Hua was under the Closed Gate. In the midst of all that, how could he not be thankful that he came all the way from the distant Yangtze River?

“Ugh. Some people are welcomed, and some get to carry the luggage.”

At that moment, Im So-Byeong came in through the door.

“Green Forest King?”

“You are here?”

Hyun Young greeted Im So-Byeong with a bright smile.

“It has been a while, elder.”

“Have you been well?”

“It is nothing... I think I can answer that after the elder looks at all the ledgers I brought along. If that passes, then nothing bad will happen.”

“Hahaha. Then, of course, there is nothing wrong.”

Hyun Young grabbed the hand of Im So-Byeong, who was joking around. There was clear trust in that gesture.

“That.”

At that moment, Chung Myung slowly approached Tang Gunak, his eyes fixed on the bottle in his hand.

“Seems like a very good alcohol...”

The color of the alcohol and the landscape painting on it felt so luxurious. There was no way that the alcohol in the bottle could be ordinary.

“Of course, it is a good one.”

Tang Gunak nodded with a faint smile.

“I brought this because I carefully selected it to have a drink with a friend I haven’t met in a long time.”

“Kuak. That is nice to hear.”

Just as Chung Myung was about to joke, Tang Gunak’s eyes darkened slightly.

“But...”

“Uh?”

“There is something I need to confirm before that.”

Tang Gunak, who stepped back slightly from Hyun Jong, stood up as he looked at Chung Myung. A serious force flowed from his body.

“Hmph.”

“...”

The faces of the Mount Hua disciples hardened in an instant.

An incredible sense of pressure, befitting the position of the family Lord of the Sichuan Tang family, weighed on him.

“A friend who has been in hiding for years without contacting me...”

“....”

“I should first confirm what he has gained during that time so that the taste of the alcohol comes alive. What do you think?”

Tang Gunak’s sharp eyes fell on Chung Myung. Tang Gunak’s qi, which hadn’t been fully suppressed, was worthy of its reputation.

However, Chung Myung, who received that gaze, just smiled even though he was directly hit by it.

“Confirmation, huh....”

Chung Myung, who slightly raised his lips, answered while looking straight at his long-awaited close friend.

“If you want to confirm that, one bottle of alcohol wouldn’t be enough.”

At that moment, a smile like Chung Myung’s appeared on Tang Gunak’s lips.

“So suddenly?”

“Woah, this is...”

The disciples of Mount Hua stepped back in a hurry.

If Chung Myung and Tang Gunak were to spar, it would be difficult to handle the aftereffects, even if it wasn’t a life-and-death battle.

But, along with the concern about being swept away, their eyes began to show a clear sense of anticipation.

Tang Gunak.

Now that he had become so familiar with Mount Hua, there were many times when they began to not feel his majestic presence. But, he was the lord of the Sichuan Tang family, which was one of the most powerful families among the Five Great Families.

Needless to say, his position in the murim world was high. Still, the weight of the name Tang Gunak was even stronger than his title as the head of the Sichuan Tang family.

Poison King, Tang Gunak.

His name was by no means inferior to the leaders of the Nine Great Sects who showed their power in the Yangtze River. No, in some ways, he was even evaluated as being stronger than them.

That person was now showing his power in front of Chung Myung without hiding it.

Even though Chung Myung was called Mount Hua’s Sword Saint and was evaluated as a potential leader of the next generation’s Murim, they still could not dare to compare him to Tang Gunak.

And yet, none of Mount Hua’s disciples who were watching this mismatched fight looked worried about Chung Myung. They just looked ahead to the outcome of this spar with excited eyes.

What was in their eyes was simply their unwavering belief in Chung Myung.

‘Such a new feeling.’

Baek Cheon’s tightly shut lips trembled slightly.

The sight of Chung Myung and Tang Gunak facing each other overlapped with the sight he had seen in the past. The two people who had met at Jo Gul’s house. And the ten-second fight that happened.

That was the beginning of their ties.

However, Baek Cheon knew. The fight at that time couldn’t be called a proper fight. Although Tang Pae had stepped in and ruined the spar, it was a fight that had started with the rule that if Chung Myung could block ten moves, then Mount Hua would be recognized as the victor.

But now, the two faced each other without any conditions, unlike back then.

“... isn’t it too rushed? No matter what, the sect leader is still here.”

When Yoon Jong spoke in a slightly timid tone, Baek Cheon shook his head and replied in a firm tone.

“I am sure the sect leader will understand.”

“But... the closer you are to each other, the more polite you should be, right? Saying this as soon as you meet.”

“You are not wrong. But...”

Baek Cheon spoke clearly without taking his eyes off the two of them.

“There are times when that happens. There are times when politeness isn’t necessary.”

The feeling of the flowing air touching his fingertips was vivid.

Through that feeling, Tang Gunak, who was certain that his concentration had reached its peak, stared blankly at Chung Myung standing across from him.

He desperately calmed down, his lips curling into a smile.

‘Life is so unpredictable.’

When he first met Chung Myung in Sichuan, would he have ever imagined that he would engage in a duel with a young swordsman who was dazzling like thunder?

Watching Chung Myung so effortlessly accept his presence and qi, he felt proud and challenged at the same time.

He must have gotten stronger.

There was no doubt about that.

That young swordsman had always done things beyond Tang Gunak’s expectations. So, of course, in the past three years, he must have gotten stronger to shatter his expectations, too.

That was why he wanted to know.

How much stronger had this young friend of his become? Whether Tang Gunak could still reach his limit or not.

It was at the moment when the rising concentration pulled the muscles of his entire body taut.

“Ah, so scary.”

“...”

Unlike him, who was full of qi, Chung Myung was acting coquettishly in a relaxed manner.

“You don’t have to act so scary. It is just a fight.”

“... can I do so?”

“Yes. You can.”

Tang Gunak, who was staring at Chung Myung, speaking without losing his composure, smiled faintly.

“I have a question.”

“... Uh?”

“Do you think there is anyone who can show such insolence in front of me and still live?”

“....”

Chung Myung’s eyes widened slightly, but soon, those eyes curved down.

Chung Myung answered with a sly smile.

“Yes.”

“... what?”

“There must be. Isn’t there at least one person?”

“...”

“Or I could be the first.”

The moment that short conversation ended, Chung Myung and Tang Gunak looked at each other and smiled.

The conversation they were having now was the same conversation they had when they first met at the merchant manor in the past.

Yes. Chung Myung was indeed the first person to act insolently to Tang Gunak, survive, and even become his friend.

“How? Will you do ten moves this time?”

“... I don’t think even a hundred moves would be enough.”

“That guy who is the head of the Tang family seems to be joking too much.”

“Because I am the head of the Tang family, I cannot say a thousand moves out of pride.”

“... you have turned a lot more easygoing.”

“Thanks to who?”

Tang Gunak smiled faintly.

It wasn’t a joke. It felt like a thousand moves wouldn’t be enough. He had been trying to suppress Chung Myung by increasing his qi for a while, but he just couldn’t seem to sense Chung Myung’s qi fighting back.

‘It feels like pushing down flowing water.’

He wanted to know.

The fierce battle on the Yangtze River.

Chung Myung survived even after facing Jang Ilso. That fact alone earned him a reputation that shook the world of Murim.

But he knew it, and Chung Myung knew it, too. The fact that he was able to stand up to Jang Ilso didn’t mean he was as strong as Jang Ilso.

Then?

What about Chung Myung now, not Chung Myung back then?

Did Chung Myung now have the strength to face Jang Ilso and the Four Precepts Alliance?

‘We will find out when we check.’

A dagger was pulled out from Tang Gunak’s sleeve. A sharp, flowing qi emanated from Tang Gunak’s body as he held the dagger between his index and middle fingers.

And at that moment.

Srrng.

Chung Myung slowly pulled out the Dark Fragrant Plum Blossom Sword. He held the sword, shining in the sunlight, facing down and took a stance.

A gentle stance that didn’t show an ounce of pressure. However, the moment he saw this, Tang Gunak felt strangely out of breath.

It was just a low-level movement, nothing special. But it was definitely different from the form Tang Gunak had seen so far. He couldn’t tell what was different, but it was definitely different.

Tang Gunak’s lips twitched.

He thought about saying, ‘Be careful.’ But he stopped himself. The person standing in front of him didn’t necessarily need such consideration.

Kuaak.

Tang Gunak’s fingers gripped the dagger that was stuck between them as if it would break.

Once, Tang Bo used the Soul Chasing Dagger.

The moment qi, which surged from his dantian, was infused, the dagger burst out with a large aura.

Tang Gunak’s clear-eyed gaze met in the air.

Clank.

The Soul Chasing Dagger that Tang Gunak had been holding suddenly appeared in the air.

Paaang! Paaang!

At the same time, two loud noises erupted, reverberating all around.

Kwak Hwi opened his eyes wide.

‘What is this?’

Even though he was clearly watching it happen in front of him, he couldn’t figure out what was happening.

‘N-no. Wait... so the two of them were facing each other, and suddenly the dagger appeared from above, and a sound burst out?’

Two things were different from the start.

All that was there was the form of Tang Gunak with his hand extended and Chung Myung holding the sword diagonally.

“No...”

A voice like salvation came to Kwak Hwi’s ears, who was confused because he couldn’t understand at all.

“... you are insane.”

“Hurry up.”

“I have finally seen this in my life,”

When he turned his gaze, Baek Cheon, Yu Yiseol, and Jo Gul were shaking their heads with exhausted faces. Thinking that they seemed to understand something, Kwak Hwi said.

“What happened?”

“Just something obvious.”

“Huh?”

Baek Cheon calmly said.

“The attacker swung the sword, and the defender just blocked it.”

“No...”

“But.”

Baek Cheon bit his lip and said.

“The process was so fast that the sound came late.”

“...”

“Isn’t it simple?”

Kwak Hwi’s face turned pale.

So... right now, in front of his eyes, Tang Gunak made his attack, and Chung Myung hit it and bounced it back, but the process was so fast that all he could see was the knife floating in the air?

‘What kind of fight is this?’

Kwak Hwi had also gained great confidence through their training. But the situation unfolding in front of his eyes had destroyed the confidence he had worked so hard to build up.

It was just different. Could there be a scene that fits that phrase so well?

“Kwak Hwi.”

“Huh? Ah... yes, sasuk!”

“Don’t miss a single moment of this duel.”

“...”

“It will be helpful to you someday. Just like we did.”

“I understand.”

The disciples of Mount Hua and Kwak Hwi, who had been lost in confusion, opened their eyes brightly at those words.

“Hmmm.”

Chung Myung lightly brushed his sword. The dull pain in his wrist brought about a subtle feeling.

“Doesn’t the name Poison King seem a bit discolored?”

Tak.

Tang Gunak shrugged his shoulders after retrieving the dagger that was spinning in the air.

“Thanks to whom?”

“Tsk. I gave you too much freedom.”

Chung Myung was pleased.

Of course, he knew that Tang Gunak had never given his all in their past duel. It would be humiliating for a proud warrior like Tang Gunak to exert his full strength against a mere third-generation disciple.

But even considering that, the power and speed of his dagger had surpassed Tang Gunak’s power from the past.

‘It is enough to make me think of the past.’

It was something that made him recall Tang Bo.

And this situation meant one thing.

Since his visit to the Tang Family in the past, Tang Gunak had focused solely on refining his skills, not on poison. It meant he had been honing himself tirelessly over the years without taking it easy.

In order to overcome the limitations of using poison.

“... it couldn’t have been easy.”

Originally, changing the fundamentals was a painful process that could not be compared to normal cultivation.

However, Tang Gunak shook his head as if it was nothing.

“It wasn’t just Mount Hua.”

“... uh?”

When Chung Myung tilted his head at the puzzling words, Tang Gunak spoke in a calm but firm tone.

“From the perspective of Mount Hua, which is advancing ahead of everything in the world, everything else must have seemed to be at a standstill.”

“...”

“But we are not just going to watch Mount Hua develop from our place. We are moving ahead slowly but surely, one step at a time.”

Tang Gunak grabbed his sword.

“So I don’t have any intention of giving up yet. It is my job to destroy the Four Sects Alliance, not yours.”

Chung Myung’s lips twitched.

It was an expression he made while trying to suppress the laughter that was escaping from delight.

-I told you that I will kill the bastard called the Heavenly Demon! I told you, hyung! Just watch from behind!

“Anyway....”

Blood was indeed thicker than water?

Chung Myung slowly lowered his sword.

What Tang Gunak said now was the best thing a warrior and a comrade could say.

Then...

Chung Myung, who was smiling, gripped his sword tightly. The green thread hanging from the tip of his sword swayed lightly.

He must prove himself as a warrior and a comrade. The time he spent was not in vain!

Paat!

At that moment, the dagger thrown by Tang Gunak turned into three rays of light and flew toward Chung Myung.

The ground was a mix of broken, pure white stones and yellow soil.

Three silver lines crossed upon it.

Three daggers, each with varying speed and force, flew toward Chung Myung, tracing lines that could only be described as beautiful.

One flew straight ahead, while the other two arced in large circles to the left and right.

The speed of the daggers circling around was much faster than that of the straight-flying dagger. By the time they reached Chung Myung’s vicinity, they arrived almost simultaneously.

‘No!’

Paat!

Chung Myung swung his sword with lightning speed, knocking away the daggers from the left and right.

Kwang!

Kwaang!

The moment the sword and daggers collided, a massive shock wave swept through the land. The ground shook as if cracking, and the soil rose up.

Paat!

Yet, Chung Myung extended his sword forward without a single flaw, even amidst the great shock. The tip of the sword rushed towards the incoming dagger like a lightning bolt.

At that moment.

Swish!

The straight-flying blade instantly changed direction, soaring upward just before making contact with Chung Myung’s sword, like a poisonous snake raising its head towards its prey.

“Euk!”

“That...!”

The disciples of Mount Hua, watching the fierce battle with sweaty palms, gulped and gasped.

The sword swung horizontally, and the rising dagger seemed poised to pierce Chung Myung’s face at any moment.

But the opponent was Chung Myung.

In a situation that would have flustered anyone else, Chung Myung’s reaction was astonishingly fast. Almost simultaneously with the dagger’s change in direction, his head tilted back violently.

Swish!

The dagger passed right in front of Chung Myung’s face.

‘A-avoid it...’

And at that moment, Baek Cheon saw it.

Whirl.

The dagger, which had barely missed Chung Myung’s face, changed direction again in the air at a right angle.

“What...!”

This time, even Baek Cheon, who usually knew what was going on, couldn’t help but be shocked. Even Chung Myung seemed flustered, his eyes wide open.

Paat!

The dagger changed direction near Chung Myung’s face and flew straight at him, leaving him no time to think about avoiding it.

Kakak!

But Chung Myung had something faster than his body. His sword was quickly pulled back to block the flying dagger.

Kakakak! Kakakaka!

The dagger didn’t lose its momentum, even though it was blocked by the sword. It spun around and pushed further as if trying to pierce through the sword.

And!

Kakakak!

The dagger twisted to the side, passing right next to Chung Myung’s face.

Kwaaang!

The moment the dagger struck, the ground flipped over and shot up as if a shell had landed there.

“....”

Chung Myung slowly turned his gaze towards the completely shattered ground.

A faint red line seemed to be engraved on his cheek, but soon it gradually became clearer.

Drip.

Red blood flowed down from his clearly wounded cheek.

“... this mister?”

Chung Myung glared at Tang Gunak, gritting his teeth.

“... Are you really trying to kill me?”

Jo Gul looked on with a stiff face.

In the first place, a dagger was a weapon that could not be easily called back once released from the hand. In other words, unlike a sword, it could not be stopped even if it pierced the opponent’s heart.

Therefore, those who use dagger techniques or assassination weapons must consider the opponent’s situation as much as possible before engaging in duels, before the fight turns into a life-or-death battle where both sides aim to take each other’s lives.

But the dagger that Tang Gunak released showed no consideration for the opponent. Rather, it seemed filled with a will to take the opponent’s life.

“If the opponent wasn’t Chung Myung, he would have died for sure.”

A dagger that had once been hit flies back at the face, changing direction? If he had been in a situation like that, Jo Gul wouldn’t have even thought of responding correctly to it.

If he knew, he could respond, but if he didn’t know, he had no choice but to suffer. Doesn’t the world call such attacks’ assassin’s moves’?

That meant Tang Gunak was now firing off these attacks of assassin movements right from the moment the battle began. And this shouldn’t happen.

“Sasuk.”

“Just watch.”

But Baek Cheon, who regained his composure, said as if it was nothing serious.

“... But...!”

“Just because you use assassin techniques, it doesn’t mean you will kill the opponent. As long as there is one guarantee, it doesn’t matter how many techniques are used.”

“Guarantee?”

“Yes.”

Baek Cheon nodded lightly.

“I am certain that the opponent will definitely be able to block it without taking any damage.”

“...”

At those words, Jo Gul closed his mouth.

Baek Cheon sighed and looked back and forth between Tang Gunak and Chung Myung.

‘That’s an absurd amount of trust.’

He watched with his own eyes the incomprehensible sight of Chung Myung, who was training them, growing stronger at a faster rate than them. That is why he had no doubts regarding Chung Myung’s strength.

But how could Tang Gunak be so sure?

If he hadn’t been sure that Chung Myung would come back stronger than he thought... no, maybe the strongest, he wouldn’t have been able to start giving out the killing intent the moment they met.

At that moment, a soft voice came.

“It is fortunate.”

“Uh?”

Baek Cheon turned to Yu Yiseol.

His samae, who always had an expressionless face, mumbled lightly while fixing her gaze on the two people ahead.

“He won’t be disappointed.”

Hearing that, Baek Cheon’s mouth curled into a smile.

“That seems clear.”

He looked at Chung Myung, who was lightly wiping the blood from his cheek.

“I guarantee you, this much won’t be enough to stop that insane bastard.”

The voice was filled with confidence and trust.

But then, a perplexing voice echoed in their ears.

“Poison?”

“....”

“Poisonnnn? No, did this guy just put poison on his weapon? Ah, my head is spinning! Oh my!”

“...”

Chung Myung grabbed his head and staggered. Baek Cheon’s face paled. Jo Gul and Yoon Jong looked back at him with sullen expressions.

“... are you sure?”

“....”

“He has made promises and seen blood more than once, yet he is being so reckless.”

“...”

Baek Cheon just looked up at the distant sky without saying a word.

Chik.

The blood dripping down the finger touched the sapphire stone below, and white vapor rose. This meant that the poison mixed with blood was truly strong.

Chung Myung glared at Tang Gunak as if it was absurd.

“POISONNN?”

“What nonsense.”

Tang Gunak shrugged his shoulders as if it was nothing.

“Euk!”

Chung Myung grabbed the back of his neck, which was stiffening.

“No, are you saying you will use poison while fighting? Poison? And that too like this?”

“I didn’t mean to speak about this...”

“What?”

“Isn’t it a bit embarrassing for you to say this with your own mouth? You should have some conscience.”

“....”

Chung Myung’s mouth was wide open.

Was the person who used poison on a dagger really talking to him about conscience?

Even Chung Myung, who was devoted to leading and had a heart like a god, couldn’t overlook this...

-Ah, stop with the disgusting nonsense!

‘Ah, just be silent there! I think I will tear off all that glue on the ancestral tablet!’

Chiik.

Chung Myung’s teeth clenched as he saw the floor still sizzling.

“You want to win so badly?”

“I don’t necessarily want it, but...”

Tang Gunak smiled broadly.

“My skills are not enough to draw out all your skills, so what can I do? I have no choice but to use everything I can.”

“... Isn’t poison of no use? I said it is useless to use between skilled warriors?”

“That is wrong.”

Tang Gunak glared at Chung Myung with cold eyes.

“Of course, it is hard to defeat someone like you with poison. But at least I can make you waste your qi and stamina suppressing the poison, and if the injury gets worse, then the poison can do its work.”

“...”

“It’s just something to do. No, if it can benefit me by a single inch, wouldn’t it be strange not to use it?”

“... Uh?”

“It is obvious who would benefit more: you, who has to use all your stamina to drive out the poison, or me, who just applies a little poison before the fight?”

... Now that he heard it, it sounded plausible?

Then why didn’t that bastard Tang Bo use poison during the war? Was he an idiot?

At that moment, the conversation he had with Tang Bo flashed through Chung Myung’s mind.

-But why don’t you use poison?

-No, this annoying man! You have to have poison to use it!

-You don’t give poison to someone who came from the Tang family?

-Seriously, do you think poison just comes out when you dig the ground? When did all the poison piled up in the Tang family run out? When would the Tang family even have time to make that poison again? If they fail to make it, it will be ruined.

-You incompetent jerk.

-Be careful with those words. If you have poison to place on the dagger, even the Taoist hyung could be hit, just once... hahaha. Let’s talk about it. Don’t draw the sword. Is it because the people of Mount Hua live on grass and stuff? You don’t know jokes or what... Eikkk!

Ah... he was a moron.

“Euk!”

Chung Myung felt dizzy for a moment and quickly turned his internal qi to suppress the poison.

Chung Myung’s unique, clear, and righteous qi neutralized the poison in an instant.

Chung Myung finally caught his breath and raised the sword while chattering his teeth.

“If you come out like this, I cannot stay still.”

“No.”

Tang Gunak shook his head.

“This isn’t all.”

Tang Gunak’s hands disappeared into his sleeves and emerged again. In his right hand was another dagger, and in his left, a collection of thin, fine needles.

The blade of the dagger glowed with a pale green hue in the light.

Chung Myung’s expression hardened slightly as he realized once again that Tang Gunak was serious about this.

“Of course, I want to see how strong you have become. But you also need to know one thing.”
