“You think it is a plan?”

“Yes, a plan. Didn’t everyone get dragged into the Yangtze River three years ago with the movement of the same pirates?”

The Gentle Crow Beggar unconsciously frowned, but he wiped off the expression as if he had never shown it. His true feelings were not as such.

‘That is because they were blinded by ambition for fame and fortune and rushed to the Yangtze River without considering what was right or wrong!’

There was a lot he wanted to say, but he had no choice but to hold back. This was Shaolin, and the person in front of him was the abbot and elder of Shaolin.

“That probably won’t be the case.”

“Why? If it is Jang Ilso...”

“It would take too long to explain, but now is not the time to waste time talking about such things. In any case, everything that is happening now is most likely the Black Dragon King’s sole decision.”

At that moment, a hollow laugh escaped from the abbot’s mouth.

“Sole decision....”

It was ridiculous.

‘These two impatient people are driving the situation to hell.’

The Black Dragon King and the Emperor’s Sword.

It cannot be denied that both were significant figures with solid positions in their forces. However, that didn’t mean that the influence of those two people was great enough to shake up the situation in the Central Plains.

However, coincidentally, due to the unilateral actions of two individuals occurring simultaneously, both the Nine Great Sects and the Precepts Sects Alliance were drawn into an unwanted war at an inopportune time.

‘Is this what war is like?’

At least, that wasn’t what the abbot thought. The war he knew involved leaders clashing fiercely with their own forces.

But the current state of the world was entirely different from what he knew. Everyone was acting as they pleased. How on earth could these situations even lead to an all-out war?

“I don’t know why you are worried.”

At that moment, Peng Yeop frowned and said,

“I don’t like that the arrogant Namgung family is acting on its own, but if the situation has already unfolded, the only option left is to support them.”

However, Jong Ri-Hyung seemed to disagree with that idea.

“I think Lord Peng needs to calm down a little. If we rush to the Yangtze River now, the other Four Precepts Alliance members, not just the pirates, will have no choice but to move. Then it will be truly irreversible.”

“But if we just watch and the Four Precepts Alliance moves first, it will be too late to support. The Namgung family will collapse first.”

“I know. I know, but it isn’t something we can act on rashly.”

“How can this be rash!”

The two men’s argument quickly ignited, and the abbot’s face darkened.

Common sense dictated that the priority should be to support the Namgung family and drive out the pirates. If the Peng family and Kongtong helped, it wouldn’t be that difficult.

But what if the Four Precepts Alliance moved north, and an all-out war broke out?

Would the sects in this place really be able to deal with the Four Precepts Alliance? The Four Precepts Alliance, led by Jang Ilso?

‘We are lacking.’

If both the Nine Great Sects and the Five Great Families had supported Shaolin, there would be no need to hesitate. But right now, it was difficult to handle the Four Precepts Alliance.

Even if they somehow won, they would have to face the remnants who had been observing the situation and were planning to take on both sides.

The abbot, lost in thought, bit his lip tightly.

‘Why are you hesitating so much?’

Shame was his burden, but death was for others. Then, shouldn’t they, as Buddhists, naturally choose shame?

“Anyway...”

When he finally spoke, everyone’s eyes were on him. It seemed as if his words still held power.

“Even if we rush to the Yangtze River right now, it would be too late. By then, the decision would have been made between Namgung and the pirates.”

“Hmm. That is true.”

“Then, we will have to prepare for the aftermath.”

The abbot slowly rose from his place.

“Give me until the end of the day.”

“You said the end of the day?”

Gentle Crow Beggars looked at the abbot, puzzled. What could he change in a day in this situation?

“Yes. One day is enough.”

“Excuse me, abbot. What do you plan to do?”

“... I will head to Shaanxi.”

“S-Shaanxi? Could it be...?”

Everyone’s eyes widened. The abbot quietly nodded.

“Yes.”

His eyes were unwavering and firm.

“I must meet the Heavenly Friends Alliance and Mount Hua’s Sword Saint in person, right now.”

Wooong!

White qi swirled from the tip of the sword. The enhanced qi erupted in an instant, sweeping away the front.

Kwaang!

Namgung Hwang shouted at the top of his lungs after sweeping away the pirates rushing in from the front with a single blow.

“Black Dragon King!”

A voice like the roar of a lion.

That voice instantly broke the enemies’ will to fight and raised the morale of his allies.

“Where are you, Black Dragon King! Did you run back in fear? Come out here right now! I want to settle the score!”

Kwaang!

The steps of Namgung Hwang shook the ground.

‘That monster!’

At that terrifying force, the face of the Underworld Old Monster, Yeo Dal, who was the guardian of the pirates, turned pale.

It was known that the Namgung family pursued the path of a blade, unlike any other sword sects that used the sword, but even in the Namgung family, the sword of Namgung Hwang was known to be unique and special.

It was like firing a cannon with a sword.

‘The path of the Peng family would be gentler!’

He had already seen the sword with his own eyes once, but it felt completely different then.

He didn’t know if it was because Namgung Hwang’s martial arts had improved dramatically over the past three years, or because he was not the Black Dragon King. Yeo Dal hadn’t yet reached the level where he could know that.

“Black Dragon KINGGGGG!”

Kwaang!

“D-damn it!”

Looking at the shattered and flying pavilion, Yeo Dal eventually shouted urgently.

“Retreat! Retreat right now!”

“G-Guardian! The Black Dragon King told us to defend this place no matter what!”

“You idiots! Even if we block them, we cannot defend them! And who is going to fight Namgung Hwang? The Black Dragon King isn’t here right now!”

“T-that...!”

“Shut up and tell them to retreat immediately! Before you all die!”

If the power of the pirates had gathered there, Yeo Dal wouldn’t have chosen to retreat. No matter how great the Namgung Family was, weren’t the pirates also one of the Five Sects in Shenzhou?

In a small-scale battle between warriors, it would be difficult to defeat the Namgung family. Still, in an all-out war, the pirates could actually overwhelm them.

However, they were currently lacking such troops.

The Black Dragon King was attacking merchant ships on the Yangtze River as soon as they appeared to increase the amount of goods going to and from Plum Blossom Island. In other words, there were more ships than usual on the Yangtze River.

Naturally, the number of ships guarding the place had to be small.

“Get back! Get back immediately! Get on the ship! We are retreating to the island!”

Yeo Dal clenched his teeth.

“This damned Namgung family!”

If he gave up the dock and retreated to Plum Blossom Island, the Black Dragon King would not leave him alone. It was clear that he had failed to carry out his orders.

‘Still, it is a whole lot better than dying.’

The wrath of the Black Dragon King in the distance was more frightening than the sword of Namgung Hwang in front of him.

Kwaang!

At that moment, several more pirates were sent flying, blood-drenched by the sword of Namgung Hwang.

“Retreat! Hurry upppp!”

At his order, the pirates turned around and ran.

In fact, even before he gave the order, they had all been thinking about running away somehow.

Namgung Hwang’s skills were truly overwhelming. In the first place, it was not something that the people gathered there could deal with.

‘How could these seclusion sect bastards even move this quickly?’

The reason the Black Dragon King decided to vacate the place, even though he knew there weren’t enough troops there, was because he thought it would take at least 10 days for the sect, who could barely move after talking for a long time, to proceed right.

But who would have imagined that the Namgung Family would suddenly appear at this time?

“Sir Guardian!”

“I know!”

Yeo Dal nodded. He couldn’t hesitate any longer. If he delayed any further, he risked losing his head.

“Start the ship! Get on board immediately...”

But Yeo Dal’s eyes, as he gave orders, suddenly widened in shock.

Through the large pavilions, on a different path from Namgung Hwang, warriors were rushing in. It was the Blue Sky Swordsmen.

At the forefront, a young warrior was seen swinging his sword like lightning. At first glance, he didn’t seem like someone who could deal with them.

“Abandon the ship, abandon that ship! Damn it, we will leave that ship behind! Just jump in! Swim to the island!”

There was no time to leisurely start the ship. Those who had launched the ship early managed to board it, but among the ships anchored at the dock, only two made it to the shore.

Realizing there was no time to board a ship, the pirates rushed into the water.

“Get out of the way!”

“Don’t grab me, you moron! Let go!”

“You bastards!”

It was a state of horror beyond words.

This was true for both the warriors and soldiers. When they have orders in their minds and something to protect, they do not spare their own lives.

But the moment that control disappears, all that remains are a few hundred cowards whose most important task is to survive.

The pirates jumped into the river, pulling and pushing their comrades. Many fell as they tried to get ahead of the others, and many reflexively swung their weapons to the side.

“Ackkk!”

“T-this son of a bitch, stabbed me?”

If they had retreated in an orderly manner, they would have already escaped. Still, they delayed the situation even more by trying to get ahead just a little. And the Namgung family’s swords coldly pierced their backs.

“ACKKKK!”

“Run away! The Namgung Family is coming!”

Splash! Splash!

The pirates who had jumped into the water skillfully swam towards Plum Blossom Island, moving swiftly without looking back. However, before the others could leap into the sea, those struck by the Namgung family’s swords screamed and collapsed helplessly on the spot.

Euk.

“Hmph!”

As Namgung Hwang’s feet touched the water’s edge, the pirates split into two groups: those who had jumped into the water and escaped quickly, and those left behind without reaching the water.

Namgung Hwang’s eyes were filled with anger.

“These fucking morons!”

A landslide victory.

It was a landslide victory that couldn’t even be described as one-sided. But that fact did not bring Namgung Hwang any joy. It only increased his disgust for having made peace with such incompetent individuals.

“This is...!”

Namgung Hwang’s eyes turned to the two ships, turning their bows.

“Get out of my way!”

Namgung Hwang, who had been guarding the side, pulled back his sword and raised it powerfully above his head.

Wooong!

The thick white sword qi swirled around his sword once again.

The sword qi, which had risen a little over a foot above his head, gathered around the sword.

“N-NO!”

“JUMPPP! NOWWWWW!”

The pirates who saw the scene from the ship screamed in confusion and jumped off the ship. They had already seen what happened when the white qi gathered around the sword.

Kwaang!

Namgung Hwang, who had stepped hard on the ground, immediately lowered the sword vertically. The qi that was released shot out like a cannon and flew straight towards the ship.

Kwaaang!

The sword qi struck the ship and crushed the wood that made up the vessel. The wood that directly touched the sword qi turned to dust and scattered, and even the parts that didn’t get touched by the sword qi shattered into pieces because they couldn’t handle the force.

Kwaaaang!

In an instant, the entire ship exploded and scattered across the surface of the Yangtze River. Not only the pirates but even the Blue Sky Swordsmen were shocked by the terrifying scene.

Namgung Hwang’s gaze shifted to the ship that was still intact. However, the quick-witted pirates had already desperately escaped the ship and jumped into the Yangtze River.

“Tsk!”

Namgung Hwang, deciding that destroying that ship would be meaningless, sheathed his sword.

The sight of the pirates desperately swimming towards Plum Blossom Island, densely filling the wide Yangtze River, was unsettling.

“... damn it. That Black Dragon King!”

At this point, Namgung Hwang couldn’t help but realize that the Black Dragon King wasn’t there. The Black Dragon King was a sly man, but he wasn’t one to avoid fights. If he were present, he would have blocked Namgung Hwang’s path.

“Lord, it is a complete victory!”

“... a victory?”

Namgung Hwang looked back.

The merchants who had been hiding inside the bar, following instructions, had gathered and were cheering from behind. This meant that all the pirates who dared to set foot on the Northern land had been killed or driven off.

“Hmph!”

However, Namgung Hwang couldn’t be satisfied with this.

“What victory!”

“Huh?”

“Since when has taken back what was supposed to be yours been called a victory? Victory is something you can only claim when you make someone else’s possession your own! You cannot call it a victory just because you have defeated a thief who broke into your house!”

Namgung Myung bowed his head at those words.

“I was afraid of the pirates who could be dealt with whenever we wanted, so I kept my head down for three years...”

Namgung Hwang gritted his teeth, and his eyes lit up.

This kind of victory would not compensate for their past humiliation. There was no Black Dragon King or the elite of the Black Dragon pirates. Even if he killed a thousand of those fools, he wouldn’t feel the slightest satisfaction.

“Prepare the ship!”

“L-Lord?”

“Excuse me!”

Namgung Hwang’s chin pointed towards the island in the middle of the Yangtze River.

“Is that the Northern Land? Southern Land?”

“...t-that.”

When Namgung Myung couldn’t answer, Namgung Do-Wei, who was standing next to him, spoke instead.

“That isn’t the Northern Land, but it doesn’t mean it’s the Southern Land either. It’s just an island.”

“Yes!”

Namgung Hwang shouted in a booming voice.

“No matter how mistakenly it was made, a treaty is a treaty. The Namgung family will not set foot on the Southern Land before the treaty’s deadline is met. It means that the Namgung family will definitely keep their word!”

“Yes, my Lord.”

“But that is not the Southern Land!”

Namgung Hwang’s eyes flared with intensity.

“Prepare the ship. I cannot stand to watch those filthy thugs set up camp there and aim for the Northern Lands any longer. I will land on the island myself and eliminate those fools!”

“L-Lord. That is too dangerous. Please reconsider.”

Namgung Myung said hurriedly.

“As you can see, that is an island. We have to cross the waters to get there.”

“So?”

“In other words, if we enter the island, we might be isolated. Support from our allies will become difficult, and it is an easy location for the pirates to attack us.”

“Who doesn’t know that?”

“Uh?”

Namgung Hwang looked at Namgung Myung with seething eyes.

“Mount Hua has protected that land for three whole years. That island is not part of the treaty and can be attacked at any time!”

“T-that is...”

“Surely you are not saying what Mount Hua can do... no, what the Green Forest, not Mount Hua, could do, we, the Namgung family, cannot do?”

“I-I absolutely didn’t mean that, but...!”

“Then get it all ready.”

“L-Lord, just listen to me once...”

Namgung Myung tried to stop him again, but Namgung Hwang clicked his tongue with a displeased look.

“I am not so stupid as to not know what you are thinking. I am not losing my mind and just going at you without thinking.”

“...”

“Don’t you know? There is no Black Dragon King in that place now. And there are no Black Dragon pirates. That means it is an uninhabited island. If we don’t do it now, it will be ten times harder to conquer that island.”

“That is true, but...”

“If you don’t move when the chance comes, you will have to put in several times more effort later. Now is the time to move!”

Namgung Myung bit his lip and nodded. Namgung Hwang’s words were not wrong.

“Understood, Lord!”

“Let us start the ship! I will take over that island!”

“Yes!”

Namgung Hwang’s fierce eyes turned to the Plum Blossom Island.

‘Black Dragon King, I will grab him by the leash. It is me, Namgung Hwang, who does it!’

A triumphant smile spread across his face.

When the Namgung family boarded the ship anchored at the dock and headed to Plum Blossom Island, the merchants cheered and jumped for joy at the sight.

“Namgung family!”

“Those damned pirates! They thought this was their world!”

Under the Black Dragon King’s orders, the pirates did not harm any merchants. However, that did not mean that the grudges against them, who had been harassing them for decades, would all be resolved.

Actually, it was refreshing to see the pirates suffer, but there was another reason why they were excited and thrilled. It was because of the Namgung family that the fear of consecutive defeats disappeared.

In the past, the Evil Faction, which had not paid much attention to them, organized the Four Precepts Alliance and pushed the Nine Great Sects into the pit of defeat, even forcing them to stay in the Northern Land.

The fear that the Northern Land would really fall into their hands if it continued was certain. The fear that they might have to live their whole lives watching those pirates was instantly dispelled by this one victory.

“Then that is right! There is no way the Nine Great Sects would tolerate it!”

“You! You bastard talking about the Nine Great Sects! The Namgung family is the best in the Five Great Families!”

“Ah! That is right!”

The hearts of the people who saw the Namgung family’s ships advancing on Plum Blossom Island swelled. The sight of the Justice Sects slaughtering the Evil Sects in one breath and protecting them was exactly what they had been hoping for over the past three years.

The Namgung family was also the sect responsible for the shame of the Yangtze River war, but that ill feeling no longer remained in the hearts of those present.

“But... is it alright to go that far? After all, it is an island.”

“What nonsense! Don’t you know what the Namgung Family is thinking? They all have their own thoughts; that’s why they are doing this!”

“Right. It has to be so?”

The merchant’s eyes lit up with hope as they watched the ship move ahead majestically.

“Proceed at full speed!”

Namgung Hwang, standing on the deck, shouted in a loud voice.

“Don’t give the pirates a chance to prepare their position. We will quickly take down the pirates while they are drowning like rats!”

“Let’s go!”

At that moment, Namgung Myung, who was looking around with a worried expression, urgently said,

“There are too many of them! It can be dangerous even if we proceed cautiously! Please slow down!”

“Do we really have to be vigilant against those insignificant pirates?”

“If you let your guard down because they are insignificant, you might end up making mistakes. Is it difficult to be vigilant against them? Or is it difficult to restore the honor of the Namgung family that will fall due to a momentary mistake?”

Namgung Hwang glared at Namgung Myung as if he were enraged.

But this time, Namgung Myung didn’t back down. Namgung Hwang, who had been glaring at Namgung Myung, slowly nodded.

“Slow down! We will proceed cautiously so that the ship does not get damaged! The Blue Sky Swordsmen are on guard against the water! Some of you jump into the water to block the pirates from approaching the ship!”

“Yes!”

The Blue Sky Swordsmen loudly reported, and some of them jumped into the water without hesitation.

“Thank you, lord!”

Namgung Myung bowed. Namgung Hwang clicked his tongue as he watched.

“Blue Sky troops leader!”

“Yes, Lord!”

Then he nodded and said,

“Do not stop those harsh words.”

“...”

“And don’t bow your head so carelessly. The Blue Sky troops leader is in a position to speak directly to the Lord. This is not something to be thankful for!”

“Yes, Lord!”

Namgung Myung’s face brightened.

This was why he had no choice but to follow Namgung Hwang. It was true that he was more reckless than anyone else, but that didn’t mean he was a tyrant.

Namgung Hwang was always open to hearing reasonable and rational words.

If something was lacking, he could fill it, and Namgung Do-wei could fill it, too. Wasn’t that what a family and a household were for?

“Lord!”

At that moment, Namgung Do-wei looked around the front and said,

“There are cannons installed on the island. I heard that the place called Plum Blossom Island is armed with the White Thunder Cannons that the White Thunder Clan used in the past.”

“White Thunder Cannons, ah.”

Namgung Hwang snorted.

“They would be helpful if there were a war between the pirates. But there is no way the cannons could reach the ships when I am guarding it!”

He continued, glaring sharply at the island.

“The only ones we need to watch out for are the Black Dragon King and the Black Dragon Pirates. If they are not on Plum Blossom Island, those pirates won’t be able to stop the Namgung family. No, even if they are, it is the same!”

What Namgung Hwang wanted wasn’t to reclaim the Northern Island.

What he truly wanted was to completely seize Plum Blossom Island and create an image of aiming a dagger at the pirates’ throats. He wanted them to experience the exact same emotions he felt when he heard that Plum Blossom Island had been taken over by the pirates.

‘There is just about one month left until the three years end.’

If that deadline passed, the Namgung family would advance to the South without looking back, to punish all of those in the Evil Four Precepts Alliance.

“What’s the situation now?”

“The pirates are not moving.”

“They say that even from under the river, no one is approaching.”

“Huh. But they don’t seem to be that many!”

Namgung Hwang smiled, the corners of his lips twisting.

Water skills?

The water skills that the pirates have learned were clearly effective underwater. However, even so, they could not completely overcome the gap in their skills.

If two individuals with the same skill, one trained in water techniques and the other not, were to fight underwater, the outcome would be obvious. However, the prowess of the Blue Sky Swordsmen could not be compared to those poorly trained in water skills.

That fact had already been proven three years ago.

Namgung Hwang’s face grew tense as he recalled the events from three years prior.

“Blue Sky Troops leader!”

“Yes, Lord!”

“Keep a vigilant watch on your surroundings! The pirates are setting up camp at a distance and could encircle us once we land on the island!”

“I will ensure it!”

“After we land on Plum Blossom Island, some of you will remain on the ship and wait! If any trouble arises, you can escape immediately!”

“Yes!”

The bitter wound from three years ago taught Namgung Hwang the importance of caution. Yet, it also meant that the wound inflicted by Jang Ilso was still causing him vivid pain.

‘Black Dragon King! Jang Ilso!’

Crack.

Namgung Hwang gritted his teeth.

He couldn’t escape the pain that occasionally gnawed at his bones. It wouldn’t be resolved unless he severed the head of the Black Dragon King, ground down Jang Ilso, and drank his blood.

“I will destroy them in one swift move!”

“Yes!”

As the ship came within sight of Plum Blossom Island.

“SHOOT!”

Kwaang!

The White Thunder Cannon fired at the ship Namgung Hwang was on. The shells, with power far beyond ordinary cannons, flew as if they intended to obliterate the ship in a single strike.

“They are flaunting their skills!”

Namgung Hwang, leaping swiftly, swung his sword like a beam of light. The white sword qi shot out, striking the flying cannonballs and altering their trajectory.

Puung!

A massive column of water erupted where the shell landed. The spreading white foam resembled fireworks.

“No need to be afraid! Advance!”

“Yes!”

The Blue Sky Swordsmen, full of spirit, shouted at the sight.

Kwaang! Kwaang!

The White Thunder Cannons continued to fire, but the shells couldn’t evade Namgung Hwang’s sword. Namgung Hwang’s eyes widened for a moment as he deflected all the flying shells.

“OHHHH!”

His sword immediately emitted sword qi.

The sword, which cut through the turbulent waters, immediately struck the White Thunder cannons installed on the riverside of Plum Blossom Island.

Kwaang!

Not only the cannon but also the pirates who had gathered to fire it were knocked out in an instant.

“Blue Sky Sword troops! Land!”

“Lord! There is still some distance!”

“We must attack when the chance is here! In case of any emergency, the Blue Sky troops leader must stay on the ship and secure a retreat route!”

“Yes!”

“Do-wei!”

“Lead the Blue Sky Sword troops and follow me! I will charge in one breath!”

“Yes!”

Namgung Hwang kicked off the bow and jumped into the water.

Paaat!

As soon as his feet touched the water, he kicked again and advanced.

He crossed the waters.

His great agility and footwork made his huge body and the fierce sword seem insignificant. It was enough to make one wonder if he was not kicking water, but solid ground.

The pirates were lost in thought and panicked at the sight of Namgung Hwang running across the water.

It was an extreme sight, demonstrating the influence a single great warrior could have on a battlefield.

And!

Kwaang!

Namgung Hwang kicked the water again and jumped, landing on the ground of Plum Blossom Island as if a shell had fallen. The pirates couldn’t run away and froze, staring blankly at the warrior in front of them.

“Ahh... Ahh...”

Namgung Hwang stared at the pirates with his sword hanging down. There was a killing intent in his eyes, suggesting he could tear them apart at any moment, but he didn’t move.

Perhaps it was just an act that gave the pirates a chance to prepare. However, Namgung Hwang’s actions definitely had a clear effect.

The fact that the pirates were losing their will to resist as if they were scared, was proof of this.

“I am certain.”

Namgung Hwang’s booming voice spread through Plum Blossom Island.

“From this moment on, no one who sets foot on the island and holds out will survive. This is the last mercy I give to you, who only take orders! Get off this island now!”

Swish!

At that moment, the Blue Sky Sword troops, who had jumped off the ship following Namgung Hwang, set foot on Plum Blossom Island, led by Namgung Do-wei. The sight of the Blue Sky Sword troops lined up behind Namgung Hwang, cutting through the waves of the river, was majestic to witness.

“Namgung Do-wei!”

“Yes, lord!”

“The warning is done! Kill the pirates! Leave no one on the island except those who use Namgung’s name!”

“Yes!”

Mountain Severing Sword, Namgung Do-wei, took the lead and charged. The moment the Blue Sky Sword troops, who had all drawn their swords, followed him, the sanity of the pirates, which had barely held on until then, broke.

“Eu, ahhhhh!”

“T-this is a dog’s death now!”

“J-Jump into the water!”

They didn’t even think about fighting back and started to run away, turning their backs.

“Stop! You bastards! Will you not stop right now! How can the warriors of the pirates turn their backs in front of the enemy! You people are worse than thugs! Stop right now!”

Yeo Dal shouted in anger until blood rushed to his throat, but his voice didn’t seem to reach the ears of the pirates.

This was perhaps natural. No matter how much he was the guardian of the Yangtze River pirates, he was ultimately no different from a firefly in front of the sun compared to the Emperor’s Sword, Namgung Hwang.

There was no way a pirate would trust him and fight against Namgung Hwang. It was a path that couldn’t be chosen without losing one’s sanity.

“This... this...”

Yeo Dal’s eyes trembled as if he were shocked.

If even this place were taken, the Black Dragon King would surely tear him to pieces. However, if he held out now, he would surely be torn to pieces by Namgung Hwang’s hands and not the Black Dragon King’s.

He had no choice but to choose between a quick death and a slightly slower one. It was natural that he couldn’t move easily.

“Namgung Hwang! Namgung HWANNGG! Do you know what you are doing?”

Unable to choose anything, Yeo Dal vented his anger at Namgung Hwang.

“The Black Dragon King will be enraged! He will be very angry. Do you realize what that means? Your actions will stain this river with blood....”

At that moment, Namgung Hwang flew over in one breath and swung his sword over Yeo Dal’s head.

Kwaaang!

Yeo Dal fell, spraying blood.

If he were his usual self, even if the opponent was Namgung Hwang, he wouldn’t have fallen to the ground like that. But his will to fight had already been broken, and he couldn’t even properly fight back, let alone defend himself.

“The Black Dragon King’s rage?”

Namgung Hwang approached Yeo Dal, who was squirming, gritting his teeth.

“You should have thought about handling my anger before that! I have been angry for three years now.”

“Kuak... that moron...”

Yeo Dal’s eyes were bloodshot.

“The Hegemony Lord will definitely take your head. You shouldn’t have done something like...”

Slash!

Yeo Dal’s head eventually rolled to the ground.

“Just keep talking. If you can, that is.”

Namgung Hwang, who had cut off Yeo Dal’s head in one breath, raised his head without regret and looked around Plum Blossom Island.

“Huh!”

The anger of the Black Dragon King? That was exactly what he had hoped for.

“It doesn’t matter who the opponent is. Those Evil Sect bastards will now tremble at the mere name of the Namgung family!”

That day, the Blue Sky Namgung Family drove out the pirates and completely occupied Plum Blossom Island.

The yard of Mount Hua was filled with people.

“Ah, I told you not to push!”

“No, this man? You are the one who got in the way and are blaming others now!”

“Got in the way? I have been waiting in line since dawn!”

“Dawn? What nonsense? I have been here since last night, soaked in the night dew! You came so late at dawn and are pretending to have come early now! You are not even a person who can be handled!”

“T-that cannot be! Don’t bluff...!”

As the two people tried to grab each other, those standing nearby stopped them.

“Are these people insane? Where do they think they are standing?”

“This is Mount Hua, Mount Hua! Do you think you are going to get hit by lightning or what!”

“If we get kicked out because of you, I will push you off the cliff!”

Yoon Jong, who saw this scene while sweeping the training ground, mumbled with a blank face.

“Gul.”

“Yes, sahyung.”

“Why did all of them come here like that? What are they here for?”

“No, how can a person who aids the Sect leader not know that? Aren’t they the people who came to show their faces after hearing that Mount Hua has lifted the Closed Gate?”

“... like that?”

“Not that many... well, they are indeed a lot.”

Jo Gul coughed in vain as he looked at the line of people, which seemed endless beyond the gate.

“If you think about it, it isn’t that shocking. Even before the gates were opened, there were a lot of visitors to Mount Hua.”

“That is true.”

“But since people I haven’t seen in three years are stopping by to say their greetings, this is just natural. Furthermore, Mount Hua’s status has also risen compared to before.”

Even before the Closed Gate was implemented, Mount Hua had almost taken over the position of the representative sect in Shaanxi. And by blocking the invasion of the Evil Sect this time, they had almost firmly established that position.

Since public opinion suggested that even if Southern Edge had intervened, it wouldn’t have been easy to turn the situation around, all the influential people in Shaanxi were eager to get into the good graces of Mount Hua.

Even outsiders who wanted to align themselves with the Heavenly Friends Alliance were coming from distant lands, so it was clear that the line wouldn’t shorten as the days passed; rather, it would grow longer.

“The world really is like that...”

Jo Gul chuckled as he watched Yoon Jong shake his head.

“I don’t know about other people, but you have to get used to it, sahyung. When you become the leader of the sect, you will have to deal with those people.”

“...”

Yoon Jong looked at the crowd gathered at the gate with an exhausted expression.

Those people? All of them?

“No, no, it isn’t just that. If it’s like this now, by the time sahyung becomes the sect leader, the line might be as long as the entrance to Mount Hua.”

“A-as if it would be that much...”

“Take comfort in your words. And that is the bare minimum.”

“B-bare minimum?”

“Of course, when sahyung takes the place of sect leader, wouldn’t it be at least 20-30 years later? The sect leader is still upright, and even sasuk would have his eyes open.”

“... your choice of words is odd, you know. Anyway.”

“What do you think Chung Myung will be like in 30 years?”

“....”

Chung Myung and not Mount Hua?

“W-what will happen to Chung Myung in 30 years...”

Yoon Jong closed his mouth.

“Until recently, he was Mount Hua’s Divine Dragon, but now he is Mount Hua’s Sword Saint. In thirty years, he might be the First of the World’s Plum Blossom Invincible Sword Emperor.”

“... you have to laugh at such jokes, but the problem is that even that doesn’t sound like a joke.”

“It isn’t a joke?”

“....”

“If you want to take that guy and act like the sect leader, even sahyung will end up having his head hurt.”

Jo Gul smiled. For the first time, Yoon Jong seriously considered handing over the sect leader position to Jo Gul.

“... The sect leader must have had a hard time too.”

Just then,

“Mount Hua Divine DRAGOOOONN! No, Mount Hua SWORD SAINTTTTT! W-we have a problem!”

Someone leaped over Mount Hua’s gate like lightning and screamed until their throat was raw.

“... We will have to deal with those people too.”

“Right.”

The two people who saw Hong Dae-Kwang, the beggar who jumped over the sect gate, shook their heads in unison.

“Plum Blossom Island is?”

“Y-yes!”

Hong Dae-Kwang gulped down the water Hyun Young had prepared for him, quenched his parched throat, and continued.

“The Namgung family has taken over Plum Blossom Island! Our cute blue pigeon had to fly thousands of miles to deliver this news!”

“... that should come under animal abuse.”

Even so, it was just a pigeon, but it flew a thousand miles with effort just because it had little blue wings. Poor thing.

Chung Myung stared intently at the blue pigeon preening its feathers on Hong Dae-Kwang’s shoulder.

Was that something that flew a thousand miles to get something delicious and didn’t even run away? If it were Baek Ah, she would have caught and eaten two wild boars on the way, then lay down with her stomach out and had a drink.

Ah, right... right. When he thought about it, it did seem useful....

“N-No!”

Hong Dae-Kwang was startled and grabbed the pigeon on his shoulder with both hands.

“What?”

“You are eyeing our pigeon right now! This is the spirit of the Beggars Union! Absolutely not!”

“Why are you acting so petty now? It is good to share nice things.”

“I said it cannot be done!”

“Tsk. Beggars have turned so harsh. It wasn’t like this in the past.”

“When is this past you are talking about?”

“There was such a time.”

That was right. It wasn’t like that back then.

Even when he hid a jug of alcohol in his pocket, he politely offered it to Chung Myung with both hands when they met. Even though Chung Myung said he was alright, the beggar insisted on giving it to him. Tsk tsk, these days, it wasn’t like...

“No, forget it.”

At that moment, Hyun Young interrupted the situation and asked again.

“The Namgung family drove out the pirates and occupied Plum Blossom Island?”

“Right, elder. The disciples in the Northern Land immediately sent up the Blue Pigeon. This is the hottest news that the main sect sent us.”

“Hmm. The Namgung family, huh...”

Hyun Young looked at Hyun Jong with a displeased expression.

“Things seem to be going badly, sect leader.”

“... I see. Could it be that the Namgung family has...”

While Hyun Jong and Hyun Young were talking, Hong Dae-Kwang glanced at Chung Myung with bright eyes.

‘What is this guy doing?’

Things had turned out this way, and he was truly shocked.

Before this even happened, Chung Myung had gone to meet him and asked about the Namgung family’s movements. While no one, not even the Beggars Union, could think of it as a variable, this guy was sitting in a faraway land alone, looking through the events that would unfold in the Yangtze River.

‘Is this something that can be done just because you are smart?’

He was confident that he knew enough about Chung Myung, but the more he learned about this strange Taoist, the more difficult it was to predict.

“Um, Branch Leader Hong.”

“Sect leader.”

Hong Dae-Kwang suddenly came to his senses and looked at Hyun Jong. It was impolite to have other thoughts in front of the sect leader, especially of Mount Hua.

“What does the Beggars Union think about this? It seems like the Four Precepts Alliance won’t stay still.”

“The Beggars Union is also on edge about the movements on the Yangtze River.”

Hong Dae-Kwang gulped dryly.

“However, it is hard to predict what will happen in the future. As you know, there are so many variables...”

If a normal war were to break out, predicting the flow wouldn’t be as difficult as one might think. Even if the number of people participating in the war increased, the individuals who actually moved the army were ultimately only a few.

However, the war in Kangho was different.

Since each sect had such a strong personality and had formed an independent force, orders didn’t work well. That was why the flow of war was completely different from what the person who planned it thought.

No one would have thought that the Namgung family, who had been living like mice for three years, would rush to the Yangtze River and defeat the pirates as soon as they heard the news... No, didn’t almost everyone think of it?

“So, the Beggars Union has asked for the opinion of Mount Hua.”

“Our opinion?”

“Yes.”

Hong Dae-Kwang’s eyes shifted to the side.

Hyun Jong, who didn’t miss that gaze, smiled bitterly. The Beggars Union, second to none when it came to information, wondered why they would bother to ask Mount Hua’s opinion.

‘Because of Chung Myung.’

Perhaps Chung Myung had said something to Hong Dae-Kwang to find out the situation at the Yangtze River without his knowledge. That must have been right.

“Chung Myung.”

“Yes?”

“How do you think the Four Precepts Alliance will act now?”

“That is, well...”

Chung Myung shrugged calmly.

“Jang Ilso must be fine with this.”

“Well, that is true.”

It was really true, but it was a useless remark.

“But actually, that isn’t very important.”

“What do you mean?”

Chung Myung snickered.

“Actually, the Four Precepts Alliance is a constant. It is obvious how those people will act now.”

“But then?”

“The variables are those people. The bald head and the hypocrites.”

“Hy-hypocrites...”

Of course, the bald head referred to Shaolin, and the hypocrites referred to Wudang.

“First, let us see how the hypocrites react.”

Chung Myung chuckled.

“Even if they sealed the gates now, they probably wouldn’t be covering their ears. If they heard that the Namgung family was beating up the Evil Sect, they would definitely be furious.”

“... of course.”

Right now, the ones who wanted to restore their honor more than anyone else was none other than Wudang. No matter how much the Namgung family was shaken by the humiliation of the Yangtze River war, how could that humiliation and anger compare to Wudang’s?

“If it were me, I would have lifted the seal on the gate and run to the Yangtze River right away... but the sect leader there is Venerable Heo Do.”

Chung Myung scratched his cheek.

Venerable Heo Do’s thoughts were so unpredictable that it was hard to foresee them.

“And more importantly, the bald...”

“Ygh.”

Hae Yeon, who was sitting in the corner, groaned.

But Chung Myung ignored Hae Yeon’s reaction and continued speaking.

“The situation will change a lot depending on how that man reacts.”

“How do you think it will turn out?”

“... my thoughts?”

“Yes. Your thoughts.”

“If you want my thoughts...”

Chung Myung had an odd expression.

“I think I know.”

“Right.”

“I hope it doesn’t happen if possible. If people had a minimum amount of shame, they wouldn’t do that. But since they are all shameless, I think they would.”

“W-what is that supposed to mean?”

“That is really hard to put into words, so if my thoughts are right, then by now...”

That was it.

“Sect leader!”

Bam!

Un Am hurriedly kicked open the door and came in, his face slightly pale.

“What is happening?”

Hyun Jong, sensing something ominous had happened, asked. Un Am opened his mouth with a perplexed expression.

But.

“Now!”

“... uh?”

Before Un Am could even finish his words, someone spoke first. Everyone’s eyes turned to the one who had opened his mouth, Chung Myung.

“In the yard.”

“...”

“The abbot of Shaolin has arrived.”

“...”

“Right?”

Un Am stared blankly at him, as if he had encountered a ghost, and nodded his head in shock.

“H-How did you know? I came running as soon as he met me.”

“Huh.”

Chung Myung turned to the ceiling and snickered.

“T-that is really shameless. A beggar who begs for money would have more shame. This bald moron. A rich monk is worse than a beggar.”

“...”

Hae Yeon, sitting in the corner, had his hair dyed red like a plum blossom.

The abbot looked at the gate of the sect in silence.

This was not the first time he had seen this gate with his own eyes, so it was familiar, yet it felt more unfamiliar than the first time.

‘Perhaps it was because my situation was different.’

Even if it was the same thing, it could not help but look different when the situation changed.

When he first visited this mountain, he didn’t have any particular regrets. Rather, he was more interested in seeing what the mountain’s sect looked like.

But now...

“Amitabha.”

The abbot chanted the Buddhist prayer to shake off the awkwardness in his heart.

‘If not me, who will go to hell now?’

This was for the sake of all people.

This wasn’t a situation where he could hesitate due to personal shame.

“Abbot.”

“Hmm.”

The abbot nodded at the voice of Beop Gye. He had no time to waste.

“Can we enter?”

Un Geom, who had been near the gate and came running after hearing the commotion, froze and looked at the abbot for a moment before nodding.

“... this way. Please enter through here.”

“Thank you, Taoist.”

Un Geom guided the abbot with a rare expression of fluster. However, this wasn’t something to blame Un Geom for. Who in the world would not be flustered when the abbot of Shaolin suddenly came without notice?

But, at that time, the abbot looked at Un Geom again for another reason.

‘Was there someone like this?’

A one-armed swordsman without any superiority complex.

The aura from him was shocking. It wasn’t hard to tell that this was a highly trained and skilled swordsman just from that feeling.

The problem was that this wasn’t in the abbot’s memory.

‘So, in just three years, did this man grow this much?’

He looked quite old. It was common knowledge that advancing one’s level rapidly became more challenging with age. Furthermore, was it true that he had made such great progress in such a short period of time with a sword, learned not as a right-handed swordsman but as a left-handed one?

Even though he had only seen one person, he could immediately understand how much Mount Hua had changed over the past three years. If this old man was like this, how much stronger must the disciples of Mount Hua be?

The abbot’s heart grew heavy.

‘Amitabha.’

Of course, it could be said that it was a good thing that Mount Hua, which belonged to the same Justice faction, was getting stronger. However, Mount Hua was a sect currently bringing a storm to the entire world. The stronger this sect became, the stronger the storm blowing across the nation would become.

Especially...

The abbot shook his head. Now wasn’t the time to think of such things.

‘My heart is full of confusion.’

Even after cultivating for so long, he was shaken by such a small thing. The unwavering determination he had pursued so much seemed all for naught.

At that moment, he saw the figure of Mount Hua’s Sect Leader, Hyun Jong, quickly approaching him.

“....”

Seeing Mount Hua running barefoot ahead of everyone else, he almost burst out laughing.

‘It is clear.’

In the past, he wouldn’t have thought it strange that Mount Hua was running out like that. If the sect leader of a declining sect heard that Shaolin’s abbot had visited, they would naturally react as such.

But was Mount Hua a declining sect? It might have been so in the past, but it wasn’t so now.

The leader of the Heavenly Friends Alliance and the lord of Shaanxi.

Mount Hua was a place where it was meaningless to even compare it to the past. The current Mount Hua didn’t even need the pretense of being in the Nine Great Sects.

But, Mount Hua’s leader, Hyun Jong, was no different from when Mount Hua was a third-rate sect that had collapsed.

‘No, it isn’t that he hasn’t changed.’

When the situation changed, one’s attitude naturally changed. Maintaining the same attitude as before, despite the change in circumstances, meant they had become a deeper person than before.

In order to not change, they must change.

Wasn’t it so in the scriptures? Color is Nothingness, and Nothingness is Color?

“Abbot, why did you come all the way here...”

Hyun Jong, who had run to him in one breath, stared blankly at the abbot as if he couldn’t continue speaking. Then, as if he had suddenly come to his senses, he bowed his head deeply and greeted him.

“Welcome.”

The abbot, who had been filled with worry, seemed a little at ease with the hospitality. Even though he knew that Hyun Jong was someone who reacted this way to everyone, regardless of his feelings.

The abbot accepted Hyun Jong’s greeting with a big smile on his face.

“I don’t know what to do with myself for being this welcomed. I shouldn’t have come to a place I wasn’t invited into.”

“What are you talking about? I welcome the abbot on behalf of Mount Hua!”

“Thank you, sect leader. I really appreciate this.”

The abbot, who had been holding a lot of hope, glanced behind Hyun Jong.

‘... Are they not here?’

The elders of Mount Hua whom he knew were there, but the person he was most concerned about was nowhere to be found. Instead, the disciples of Mount Hua, who had come running after hearing the commotion, turned their heads and whispered to each other, looking at the abbot.

“What is happening?”

“The abbot of Shaolin came here?”

“The abbot? Why is the abbot here?”

He was already used to the gazes of people looking at him strangely. From the moment he became the abbot of Shaolin, he couldn’t avoid such gazes.

But after checking the faces of those who were sending him these gazes, he couldn’t help but feel uncomfortable.

‘They are strong.’

Their faces still had their youthfulness. They were young swordsmen who had shed their youth but hadn’t yet reached maturity. However, the qi he felt from those young swordsmen was extremely sharp and, at the same time, infinitely serious.

The momentum was something he had never felt before, even in the Namgung family or the Wudang.

Now, he didn’t know what to say to describe Mount Hua. The words ‘The Wall of the Upper World’ and ‘Look Once Again Clearly’ seemed inadequate to describe their growth.

What impressed the abbot even more was their behavior. The way they hung around him was no different from kids in a market.

Innocent?

No.

In Taoism, this kind of behavior was described as ‘pure.’ Not being formal, not being overly polite, and being honest about one’s feelings. That was the attitude that Taoists who wanted to resemble nature should have.

‘Strength and purity.’

It seemed that he could understand why Mount Hua threatened Wudang’s position just by looking at them there.

But... the abbot knew better than anyone that it wasn’t all.

“I greet the abbot.”

“I greet the abbot.”

The abbot nodded quietly as he watched the people who were showing him respect.

‘The Five Swords of Mount Hua.’

They were now the warriors representing Mount Hua. Of course, their names still were called among the upcoming great warriors. But one day, they would become the names representing the true warriors of the world.

“Nice to meet you, Taoist Baek Cheon.”

“You know my name?”

“How could I not know Mount Hua’s Righteous Sword?”

“... It is an honor.”

Baek Cheon bowed his head with impeccable form.

A figure that embodied the phrase ‘Good Looking and Dignified’ better than anyone else. Even though he was the abbot of Shaolin, brimming with talent, at this moment, he felt a certain jealousy towards Hyun Jong.

“... you are working hard.”

After receiving Baek Cheon’s greeting, the abbot turned to the person standing behind him. To the person who had once been his pride, who could have been like Baek Cheon to the abbot.

The abbot smiled brightly at Hae Yeon, who seemed to have mixed feelings within him.

“Did you learn a lot?”

“Abbot...”

The abbot, who had been quietly looking at Hae Yeon’s face, suddenly lowered his gaze and stared at the murim uniform he was wearing.

‘Murim robes of Mount Hua.’

He could guess the situation they were in. However, if Hae Yeon’s heart had not wavered even a little, he wouldn’t have worn the clothes of another sect, even if it meant being in rags.

“I have a lot to talk to you about. But now isn’t the time, so let us continue the talk later.”

“... yes, abbot.”

As Hae Yeon silently lowered his head, Hyun Jong stepped ahead to protect Hae Yeon with a flustered look.

“Abbot, there were a few circumstances here... it was all my fault for not paying the right attention.”

“Do not be like that, sect leader. I don’t mean to scold the child. I just have a lot to talk about with my disciple, who has been away from the main sect for a long time.”

“If that is the case, then...”

Hyun Jong nodded, and the abbot smiled broadly and changed the topic.

“I came running all the way here because there was a matter that needed to be discussed in detail.”

“M-My mind has also been in a mad state. I am sorry, abbot. Do come this way.”

“Yes, sect leader.”

Hyun Jong quickly guided the abbot.

Walking through the slightly noisy Mount Hua, the abbot observed the atmosphere closely. Compared to the quiet Shaolin, this was a place of noise and life.

“This must be the atmosphere of a sect that is advancing.”

Before he could organize his thoughts, Hyun Jong stood in front of the Mount Hua Sect leader’s residence with the abbot.

“This is the place. I apologize for not being prepared to receive guests.”

“How can an uninvited guest even think such a thing? I would be grateful even for a glass of lukewarm water.”

“Please, drink.”

Before entering the room, the first thing that caught the abbot’s attention was the pair of shoes placed at the front door.

They were a little small for Hyun Jong to use.

This meant that there was someone in the room who didn’t show themselves in the presence of the abbot. The abbot knew who the person was without even seeing them.

“... Amitabha.”

The abbot quietly prayed scriptures that no one could hear. The person he had rushed to Mount Hua for, and truly wanted to meet, was there.

The abbot, who had been staring blankly at the door, finally entered the room. As soon as he crossed the threshold, the first thing that came into view was the person sitting with his back to the corner.

Chung Myung had not changed much. He could be recognized at a glance.

No, rather, there was enough change to notice something different. If he had to pinpoint it, Chung Myung seemed a little taller and a little more solid, but he was no different from what he had seen before.

Even the atmosphere and the look in his eyes.

Chung Myung did not show any particular reaction even though he saw the abbot enter. He just stared at the abbot with round eyes.

The two looked at each other for a moment.

Breaking the awkward silence, the abbot smiled first and said,

“How have you been, young Taoist? No, the word ‘young’ doesn’t really fit you anymore.”

His tone was very gentle as if he were speaking to someone he was close with.

Chung Myung smiled brightly and answered.

“Yes. Thanks to you, I have been doing well.”

“Is that so? I didn’t think I did anything for you.”

“Ehh. Why didn’t you do anything? Do you know how much you did for me?”

“I did?”

“Yes.”

Chung Myung smiled brightly. But while his mouth formed a smile, his eyes were not smiling at all.

“Since you are holding a service to make me fail so badly, shouldn’t I live well just to make you feel wronged? Thanks to you, I have been very, very well.”

The disciples of Mount Hua, who had just entered the room, opened their mouths.

‘Th... that...!’

‘Insane bastard.’

Seeing Chung Myung attack the abbot with words right from the start, they were already worried about how much this place would end up like a sheet of thin ice to walk on.

Cold sweat began to run down their backs.

An awkward atmosphere lingered.

No, in fact, the current atmosphere was closer to cold than awkwardness.

‘That insane bastard...’

Of course, in the past, Chung Myung wasn’t particularly polite to the abbot. But back then, the relationship between Shaolin and Mount Hua wasn’t so bad.

Baek Cheon bit his lip tightly.

‘And that gap has turned extreme.’

A tiger didn’t necessarily get angry because a pup barked. It just ignored it for a while and then bit it to death when it got on its nerves. In other words, it was no different from saying that a pup was guaranteed a chance to tease a tiger, too.

But now?

Would Chung Myung really look like a pup in the abbot’s eyes now?

Baek Cheon thought, absolutely not. No matter how much the abbot was the sect leader of Shaolin and the unrivaled leader in the murim world, he couldn’t treat Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, Chung Myung, like a pup.

The current status of Chung Myung was more than enough to make even the abbot feel uncomfortable.

So, the current situation was bound to be like a sheet of thin ice.

But, fortunately, or unfortunately, it wasn’t the abbot who reacted to those words, but Beop Gye, who followed behind the abbot and entered.

“Look here, Taoist.”

Beop Gye glared at Chung Myung with cold eyes and said.

“There is a minimum courtesy required when dealing with an elder of the same side. Don’t they teach that in Mount Hua?”

If it were the usual Beop Gye, he would have never stopped at this level. He was the head of the Discipline Hall, who dealt with Shaolin’s monks.

However, he knew why the abbot had come all this way, so he cut down the words.

But Chung Myung’s reaction made his effort meaningless.

“Courtesy?”

Chung Myung chuckled.

“Ah, courtesy is nice. I like it too.”

Then he looked at Beop Gye and said.

“But since you live in the mountains, you only know one thing and not the other.”

“... What do you mean I don’t know?”

“It is basic to be polite to your superiors and elders, but there is another basic in the murim world.”

“... What is that?”

Chung Myung stared at the two uninvited guests and twisted the corners of his mouth.

“It is that those who feel unfortunate need to be courteous.”

“...”

Beop Gye’s mouth shut.

“Am I wrong?”

Chung Myung’s crooked smile pierced into Beop Gye’s eyes. Anger surged in him for a moment, but even if he was Beop Gye, he couldn’t let go of his anger.

It was also because the abbot was right in front of him...

‘Mount Hua...’

That name suppressed Beop Gye’s urge to act rashly. Before he knew it, the weight of Mount Hua had become just as heavy inside him.

A calm voice reached Beop Gye’s ears as he was torn between his rising anger and the weight he felt.

“Right, young Taoist, no, Mount Hua’s Sword Saint. As you said, the one in need has to be courteous.”

“A-Abbot.”

The abbot smiled broadly. That smile eased the tense atmosphere a little.

“I was worried that your personality might have changed since it has been years since we last met, but it seems like you haven’t changed at all.”

“They say that when a person changes, it is time to die. Now that I think about it, you seem to have changed a little, abbot?”

“...”

The abbot’s mouth went half open at those words.

What on earth should he do now?

“This guy!”

Fortunately, the abbot had someone he could call an ally. If he was someone from whom this young Taoist emerged, then so be it.

Hyun Jong, whose face twisted into a grimace, gave a loud scolding.

“He is the abbot of Shaolin. Can you not be polite?”

“No, how can you be polite anymore, sect leader?”

But Chung Myung asked back as if he found it all absurd. Hyun Jong flinched.

“... How?”

“Yes!”

“That is?”

“Of course!”

Chung Myung said, pounding his chest.

“But since he is the leader of Shaolin, he should be greeted and talked to. If not, oh my, this place would be a sea of blood right now. Someone might die, someone will die! Now that I think about it, didn’t someone say it was time for death a little while back?”

“No one said anything like that! Get away now!”

“Ah, that is strange. I think someone did say it.”

“Will you not go now, you brat!”

“Yes, yes.”

Chung Myung regretfully smacked his lips and got up from his place, slowly heading to the back. Even as he did so, he didn’t forget to glance at the abbot as if he wanted to get angry and fight him right away.

“... Amitabha.”

The abbot uttered a short prayer to calm down his emotions, which were hard to explain.

How could the demon, who was casually picking fights with the head of the Shaolin, listen so well to Hyun Jong?

Hyun Jong spoke with an embarrassed look.

“I am sorry, abbot. I took him in as a disciple, but I couldn’t teach him right...”

“No, why are you apologizing to him...!”

“Will you not be quiet!”

When Hyun Jong opened his eyes widely and grabbed the plate as if he were going to throw it, Chung Myung flinched and dug into the corner. He leaned his head against the wall, mumbling something, but Hyun Jong desperately ignored it.

‘If I send him away, the abbot will feel uncomfortable, and if I leave him alone, my insides will burst out.’

It was driving Hyun Jong insane because he couldn’t do a thing there.

“Sect leader.”

At that moment, the abbot smiled brightly and opened his mouth.

“I am just jealous. How much more secure must the sect leader be with such great descendants in Mount Hua?”

“They are just troublemakers. I have to somehow turn them into Taoists before I die.”

He said that, but Hyun Jong’s face was filled with a sense of pride that he couldn’t hide. At least for this moment, the abbot couldn’t help but envy him.

“Please sit down.”

“Yes.”

As the abbot sat in the center, those who entered sat around him.

Hyun Jong looked at the two of them quietly. It was just the abbot and Beop Gye sitting in front of him, but an inexplicable weight pressed down on his shoulders. It felt like the room was filled with the presence of those two people.

‘The abbot of Shaolin.’

Didn’t they say that you had to be of sufficient rank to know the level of the opponent?

When he was struggling to save Mount Hua, he didn’t realize how great a person the abbot was. Only now, after going through so much, could he understand how great a person the abbot was, who had managed the huge sect called Shaolin without a hitch.

And how much weight he was holding.

“Ahem.”

Hyun Jong opened his mouth, coughing as if trying to shake off the presence of the abbot.

“You must have had a hard time coming this far.”

“No problem at all.”

The abbot smiled broadly and opened his mouth.

“I thought it was far because it was called Shaanxi, but when I came in a hurry this time, it didn’t take that long. If I had known sooner, I would have visited often.”

“I feel so embarrassed because those words seem to blame me for not being able to visit often. I should have come to greet you earlier, but you embarrassed me by coming here first.”

“The sect leader is one of the busiest people in the world, and I... ah, what a mistake.”

“Uh?”

The abbot rubbed the back of his head as if he had made a mistake. The moment he saw the hand touching his bald head, he thought that Chung Myung was right and tried to say something. Still, Baek Cheon’s hand, which was already waiting, immediately covered his mouth.

And thanks to that, the abbot continued what he was trying to say.

“You aren’t the sect leader. I should have referred to you as the Alliance leader, and I made a mistake by not calling you as such. Please forgive me.”

“Ah, forgive? Sect leader is enough to refer to me.”

“That cannot be. Everyone has a title that fits their position.”

Then Hyun Young, who was next to him, opened his eyes slightly.

‘That monk...’

The fact that he insisted on going up to Mount Hua and calling Hyun Jong the Alliance Leader, instead of the Sect Leader, meant he planning to make a deal with the Alliance Leader now.

“Ahem.”

Hyun Jong, too, understood the intention and coughed.

“I need to be careful with my words and actions when I have the abbot in front of me. If I ask a question, it seems like I would rush you, and if I make small talk, it would seem like I am wasting your time.”

“Hahaha. I came here to see you, and you are taking my time.”

“Abbot.”

Hyun Jong sighed and looked at the abbot.

“If it is not rude, may I ask why you had to come to Mount Hua?”

The abbot made a subtle expression.

“To be frank...”

Then he paused for a moment and opened his mouth.

“I thought that the ties between Shaolin and Mount Hua weren’t what they used to be. There were many things going on, and there were times when it was great.”

Hyun Jong waited for the abbot’s next words with a nervous expression.

“But... I didn’t think I would be able to get a cup of tea just because of that. The plum tea that the sect leader gave me the other day was really good.”

“Ah...”

Hyun Jong’s face turned red. Now that he thought about it, the abbot of Shaolin had come as a guest, but he didn’t offer him a cup of tea and got right to the point.

He would never have made such a mistake under normal circumstances. Still, the sudden visit of Shaolin’s abbot to Mount Hua had clearly shaken him.

“I made a mistake. Un Am! Go and get the tea, hurry!”

“Yes!”

Un Am also realized his mistake and jumped up and ran outside.

“I am sorry, abbot. I was quite flustered with the sudden situation.”

“No. No. It wasn’t my mistake. I just felt that the Alliance Leader no longer thought of me like he used to.”

“How could that be? That could never be the case.”

“Then, I am fortunate. Hehehe.”

Chung Myung’s face was slightly distorted as he watched the situation unfold.

‘This snake-like bald head!’

With just a few light words, the abbot instantly took control of the atmosphere in the room and completely seized control of the situation.

Furthermore, when mentioning past events, he used the title of ‘sect leader.’ When discussing the present, he would use ‘Alliance Leader,’ pointing out that the treatment was different because the status was different.

Regardless of whether he liked the person or not, he could not help but acknowledge his ability to achieve what he wanted with just a few words. That is why he must have risen to the position of the abbot of Shaolin.

“The tea is coming soon...”

“Alliance Lord.”

The abbot opened his mouth in a soft voice.

“The tea is nice, but there is something more important.”

“What...”

“You heard the story, right? I am talking about the Yangtze River.”

The abbot spoke as if he expected Hyun Jong to know. It was just yesterday that the Namgung family occupied Plum Blossom Island in the Yangtze River.

“...Yes, I heard.”

“As I thought.”

The abbot looked back. The beggar he had clearly seen when he came here was not around.

“The situation is worse than I thought, Alliance Leader.”

“Yes. That is what I heard, too.”

“There is just one reason why I came here, Alliance Leader. Please lend me your strength.”

At that moment, the abbot bowed his head deeply to Hyun Jong on the spot. It was as if he was bowing and begging.

“W-what are you doing!”

Hyun Jong, who had been lost in thought, jumped up in shock. He was so shocked that he jumped over the table and grabbed the abbot to hold him up.

“W-why are you doing this, abbot?”

The greatest Shaolin sect’s abbot was bowing to him!

Even Hyun Jong couldn’t help but be flustered.

But Chung Myung, who had watched the process, finally let out a hollow laugh.

“Huh...”

The old snake was now wrapping its tail around the neck of Hyun Jong.

‘Is this how you act out now?’

The Abbot of Shaolin bowed his head. The sight made even the disciples of Mount Hua, who were watching, remain silent.

‘The abbot of Shaolin...’

To Mt. Hua’s sect leader.

Perhaps this was a moment to feel pride. Only a few years ago, Mount Hua had nearly collapsed, with its pavilions almost taken away. It was incredible that Hyun Jong, once merely the leader of a third-rate sect, had risen to a position where the Abbot of Shaolin bowed to him in just a few years.

Yet, no one felt pride. They were simply shocked and astonished by this unbelievable situation.

“Don’t do this, Abbot. I don’t know how to respond.”

“Alliance leader.”

The Abbot firmly grasped Hyun Jong’s hand as he tried to raise him.

“I will not deny that Shaolin committed a wrong against Mount Hua. No matter how much we lacked resources, if Mount Hua was under Closed Gates, we should have dealt with the Evil Sect moving to Shaanxi.”

“...”

“Please forgive me for not doing so.”

“... How can you call that a mistake?”

A faint groan escaped Hyun Jong’s lips.

Shaolin and Mount Hua had harbored ill feelings for a long time. The decisive incident was the one the Abbot mentioned. Until then, it could be seen as a difference in position, but this incident held malice.

If Shaolin had considered Mount Hua allies, this would never have happened.

Yet, the Abbot mentioned it and bowed his head. If someone of the highest position, like the Abbot of Shaolin, acted so humbly, even Hyun Jong had no choice.

If Hyun Jong chose to be stubborn at that moment, those hearing of the incident without knowing the circumstances would criticize him.

‘This is scary.’

It was easy to speak. The logic behind it was simple.

However, how easy would it be for the Abbot of Shaolin, the leading light of Kangho, to bow to someone? It wouldn’t be easy to simply bow, so how could it be possible to repent for a mistake by bowing to the floor?

It was an unimaginable blow. Even if the one who was hit weren’t the gentle Hyun Jong, there would be no other way to respond for anyone in that situation.

“Mount Hua doesn’t blame Shaolin. We know that Shaolin was working tirelessly day and night for the stability of Hunan, so how can we blame Shaolin?”

“... Alliance leader.”

“But... it is sad and regrettable that there are those who have fallen victim to the Evil Sect people.”

Hyun Jong let out a deep sigh.

“So please don’t do this, Abbot. I cannot accept this.”

The Abbot nodded and sat up again. Only then did Hyun Jong let go of the Abbot’s hand and sit back in his place.

At that moment, Un Am came in with a tea set.

“Sect leader.”

“Yes.”

Hyun Jong accepted the tea set, which Un Am offered with a feeling of satisfaction.

“It seems like the order has changed a little, but I will serve you tea.”

“Tea from the world’s Heavenly Friends Alliance leader, what greater luxury than this?”

“... I am grateful that you think so.”

Hyun Jong began to make the tea with a slightly pale look. His surprised heart felt a little calmer as he held the teapot.

“Here it is.”

“Thank you, alliance leader.”

The Abbot took a sip of the tea that Hyun Jong offered and smiled broadly.

“The plum tea from Mount Hua is truly the best. It seems to be superior to any tea I have ever had.”

“It is just that I couldn’t find good tea leaves like other places, so I just dried the plums which were scattered all over the mountain. How can it be compared to the famous teas around the world?”

“If it has its unique characteristics, how could it be lacking compared to the rare ones?”

The atmosphere relaxed a little.

However, those who were quick-witted knew that the Abbot was in control and leading the situation.

‘Indeed, the abbot of Shaolin...’

He didn’t particularly dominate or show off his status. He didn’t pressure anyone around, either. Nevertheless, everyone in the room couldn’t take their eyes off the Abbot’s every single move.

Tak.

The Abbot carefully put down the teacup in his hand.

“Alliance leader.”

“Go ahead, abbot.”

“As I said, the situation in the Yangtze River isn’t good.”

“... Yes.”

“The Namgung family has occupied Plum Blossom Island on their own. There is no way that the Four Sects Alliance would tolerate the Namgung family’s presence there, pointing a sword at them right in front of their land.”

Hyun Jong frowned.

“Would the situation be that risky? In fact, that island named Plum Blossom Island...”

“Yes. Mount Hua definitely occupied that island.”

Hyun Jong nodded at that. Despite that, there had been no particular problem for three years.

“But Mount Hua and the Namgung family are different places. Mount Hua had no intention of attacking the Southern Lands, but the Namgung family is definitely provoking them.”

What should have been said originally was that the Four Precepts Alliance treated the Nine Great Sects and Heavenly Friends Alliance differently.

But the Abbot did not dare to speak those words. This wasn’t a good thing for him or Mount Hua.

“The Black Dragon King who was attacked would not let it go. And... the Hegemony Lord he will definitely move. If the two main forces of the Four Precepts Alliance move, the entire Four Precepts Alliance will eventually move.”

The Abbot kept his mouth shut for a moment and looked around at the people around him. The eyes looking at each person were firm.

“If that happens, the innocent people will be those who suffer in the end.”

“...”

“I am ashamed to say this, but right now, the Nine Great Sects are not enough to control the Four Precepts Alliance. So, Alliance leader, please lend me your strength. If the Heavenly Friends Alliance joins us, the Four Precepts Alliance wouldn’t dare invade the Northern Lands easily.”

“Ugh.”

Hyun Jong frowned slightly and sank into deep thought. The word’ innocent people’ was on his mind.

He knew that Shaolin hadn’t yet revealed its true feelings. And Hyun Jong couldn’t reveal all of his true feelings to them either.

However, no matter what his hidden feelings were, Shaolin now couldn’t control the Four Precepts Alliance on its own. The damage would definitely fall on the people, not Shaolin. This was an undeniable fact.

Hyun Jong opened his mouth, which was too heavy to open.

“What I cannot get myself to understand is...”

“Yes.”

“Is it really difficult to deal with the Four Precepts Alliance with the power of the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families?”

This time, the Abbot closed his eyes for a moment.

It was something he didn’t want to say, but hiding that didn’t feel like the best option.

“It is my fault.”

“...”

Hyun Jong didn’t ask more questions. He had already heard it from Chung Myung.

‘What should we do about this?’

The situation was simple. Right now, the sects that followed Shaolin alone could not stop the Four Precepts Alliance from heading north to the Yangtze River.

The sects in Sichuan and the Northern Part of Central Plains did not want to fight and shed blood in a place that wasn’t theirs.

If the Four Precepts Alliance was really so strong that they couldn’t even touch it, they could have united as one for a show of force. But the problem was that the Four Precepts Alliance wasn’t strong enough to make everyone risk their lives.

Hyun Jong also had no desire to fight alongside Shaolin. Besides, dealing with the Four Precepts Alliance was something that had to be done.

However, everything involved a sacrifice. If others did not step forward, why would Mount Hua take the lead?

Right. If it wasn’t for the innocent people.

“Hmm.”

As Hyun Jong’s concerns deepened, the Abbot spoke again.

“It isn’t for the sake of the Nine Great Sects and Five Great Families. Please consider the people of the world who will suffer if war breaks out. The alliance leader must...”

“People of the world?”

At that moment, an odd voice interjected.

The Abbot opened his eyes, which had been tightly shut. He knew that from now on, it was the real deal.

“Right, the people of the world.”

“Ah. Shaolin is surely different. You know how to bow your head for all the people of the world. To be honest, I was a little shocked.”

Chung Myung smiled and looked at the Abbot. However, everyone there, including the Abbot, knew.

That smile was not genuine.

“But...”

As expected, Chung Myung’s smile turned into a crooked sneer.

“Don’t you think the situation is a bit ridiculous to believe such things?”

“... What do you mean?”

“In my opinion, it seems like you are the one who wants war to happen.”

“Chung Myung!”

Baek Cheon was surprised by Chung Myung’s words and snapped. Even to him, this was a statement that crossed the line.

The face of the Abbot, who maintained a gentle expression, hardened slightly.

“Can I ask why you think that?”

“It is simple. What should we do to prevent the people from being harmed?”

“... We need to stop the Four Precepts Alliance.”

“You are wrong.”

Chung Myung shook his head.

“What we have to stop is not the Four Precepts Alliance, but the war. The people aren’t suffering just because the Four Precepts Alliance exists. At least in the Northern Lands.”

“...”

“If you don’t plan on pushing into the Southern lands, the best way to protect the people is to not start a war. But you, Abbot, are only thinking about fighting and winning. Am I right?”

“They want a war, so how can I stop them?”

“Surely you are not asking that because you don’t know the answer, right, Abbot?”

Chung Myung’s chuckle grew deeper.

“If you really wanted to stop the war, you shouldn’t have rushed here. Instead, you should have gone to the Yangtze River, beaten Namgung Hwang, and dragged the Namgung family out of Plum Blossom Island.”

“....”

“If the Namgung family is satisfied with recovering the Northern land and doesn’t attack Plum Blossom Island, it won’t be a situation where one points a knife at your neck, as the Abbot mentioned. Even though the pirates were humiliated, if Plum Blossom Island is given up, they wouldn’t be able to start a war alone.”

The Abbot looked at Chung Myung with a blank expression.

“Well, I know. If that happens, Shaolin will be cursed. The Namgung family gave the land they recovered to the pirates, Shaolin is afraid of the Four Precepts Alliance and avoids fighting, Shaolin’s Abbot is a coward....”

“....”

“But.”

Chung Myung shrugged his shoulders after pausing for a moment.

“In place of being cursed, Shaolin gets to save people.”

The Abbot’s body trembled slightly. Chung Myung’s last words stabbed him sharply.

“Why? You didn’t even think of that, did you?”

The Abbot couldn’t answer. It was clear that this method had not been considered. No, it had not even existed in his mind from the start.

The reason flowed out of Chung Myung’s mouth.

“Well, of course, you hadn’t thought of it. In the Abbot’s mind, Shaolin comes first before anything else in the world. He would never have thought about Shaolin getting cursed for trying to clean up the mess the Namgung family had made. And you couldn’t stand to give out the impression that Shaolin was afraid of the Four Precepts Alliance.”

“I just...”

“No, well... I am not trying to criticize you. Of course, you could. Honestly, it isn’t very pleasant. I know it must be unfair to the Abbot. It wasn’t your fault. I completely understand it. But...”

A cold gaze fell on the Abbot’s face.

Soon, Chung Myung’s lips twisted, revealing his teeth.

“Then at least you shouldn’t have pretended to care about the people by saying things like, ‘What about the people? What about the innocent people of the world?’ You hypocrite.”

The Abbot eventually closed his eyes tightly.

But there was no way to block the voice coming through his pierced ears.

A silence so loud that one could hear a pin drop filled the room. Even though there were quite a few people present, not a single breath could be heard.

Everyone just held their breath and looked at Chung Myung and the abbot.

‘Oh my...’

Baek Cheon clenched his fists without realizing it.

He had definitely expected this to some extent.

It had already been proven many times that Chung Myung did not like the abbot, and this incident with the Evil Sect had brought that ill feeling to its peak. So they guessed that even if the opponent was the abbot, Chung Myung wouldn’t let it slide.

But...

‘No matter what, the man is still the abbot of Shaolin.’

The leader of the murim world, Shaolin.

Who among the warriors wouldn’t admire that name? Baek Cheon’s roots were from Southern Edge, but he chose to live in Mount Hua, yet Shaolin was something sacred that he couldn’t dare touch.

But the Shaolin abbot, representing that sacred place, was now silent, unable to even refute the harsh words a Taoist had said.

Who would have imagined that they would witness such a sight in their lifetime?

He knew that he should stop Chung Myung, but he couldn’t bring himself to open his mouth.

All Baek Cheon could do was hold his breath and try not to miss a single word of their conversation.

“Taoist...”

After a long silence, the abbot spoke.

“I just didn’t think about it yet. If I had thought about it in advance, I would have gone to the Yangtze River. This is just...”

“Ah.”

Chung Myung cut off the abbot’s words with a short exclamation.

“You would have gone to the Yangtze River?”

“... that...”

“You would have rushed to the Yangtze River, stopped the Namgung family from fighting the Evil Sects, handed the Plum Blossom Island back to the Evil Sect, and once again endured the humiliation of Shaolin surrendering to the Evil Sects?”

“...”

A sneer appeared on Chung Myung’s lips.

“You would?”

The abbot closed his mouth, which he had opened with difficulty.

He tried to say something, but it seemed as if someone had forcefully shut his mouth.

He attempted to explain that he would have acted out of reflex, yet he also felt the truth.

The truth is that he would never have done that.

Would Mount Hua’s Sword Saint believe something that even he couldn’t believe? The man who seemed to understand the abbot’s inner thoughts better than the abbot himself?

In the end, a hollow laugh escaped the abbot’s mouth. This wasn’t a situation where persuasion could work in the first place.

“It seems that the abbot of Shaolin only studies the principles of the Buddha and doesn’t know the ways of the world. If you do not know, I will tell you.”

Chung Myung said with great sarcasm.

“You clean up after your own actions.”

“...”

“It isn’t normal to run to someone else and cry and ask them to clean up.”

At that moment, it wasn’t the abbot but Beop Gye who couldn’t hold back his anger. It was difficult to tolerate a young Taoist speaking to the abbot of Shaolin as if the abbot were a child.

“Aren’t you being too much?”
